How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Endermite Pet
Aliases: Endermite Pet Egg, Mystery Endermite Pet
Categories: Mythic pets, Pets, Pages with auctionable items without valid auctionable item IDs, Craftable items, Templates with deprecated argument names, Confirmations needed, End Stone Collection, Mining pets, 0.7.8, Mining
[Endermite Pet]
Type: [[Mining]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): End Stone III (3) ([[End Stone]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[End Stone]] 144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) [[Egg]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: ENDERMITE
Tooltip: Mining Pet; Intelligence: +1.5➡150; Pet Luck: +0.1➡10; More Stonks Gain more exp orbs for breaking end stone and gain a +0.5➡50% chance to get an extra block dropped.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Endermite [[Pets|Pet]] is a craftable [[Mining]] [[Pets|Pet]] unlocked at [[End Stone|End Stone III (3)]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[End Stone]]|5 Minutes|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted End Stone]]|10 Minutes|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|25k [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted End Stone]] 32x [[Mite Gel]]|15 Minutes|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|32x [[Enchanted End Stone]] 64x [[Mite Gel]]|30 Minutes|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]|64x [[Enchanted End Stone]] 1x [[Mixed Mite Gel]]|1 Hour|

## Trivia
- After over 2 years of pets existing, the 0.16 update made this pet the second pet to grant [[Pet Luck]] specifically and first pet to have it as a stat and not ability
- Similar to the [[Transmission Tuner]], the Daily Commuter ability affects the abilities of [[Aspect of the End]], [[Aspect of the Void]] and the [[Etherwarp Conduit]].
- The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Endermite Pet ability Mite Bait is currently bugged, and does not function when above 100% odds of spawning an extra [[Nest Endermite]] (summoning 0 extra endermites)[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].

Pet ID: FROG
Tooltip: Foraging Pet; Speed: +0.5➡50; Fishing Speed: +0.4➡40; Respiration: +0.1➡10; Sweep: +0.1➡10; Hop Grants 0.8➡40 Foraging Fortune for 20s every time you jump. Gain Jump Boost IV while on Foraging Islands.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Frog Pet is a [[Foraging]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
A [[Rarity|COMMON]] Frog Pet can be purchased from [[Agatha]] for 20 [[Agatha's Coupon]]. Rarities up to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] are obtained through [[Kat]] by using Frog Treats purchased from [[Agatha]], while upgrading to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] requires a [[Frogspawn Bucket]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|1x [[Small Frog Treat]]|1 Hour|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|1x [[Medium Frog Treat]]|2 Hours|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|200k [[Coins]]|1x [[Large Frog Treat]]|3 Hours|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|1x [[Giant Frog Treat]]|4 Hours|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|1x [[Frogspawn Bucket]]|1 Hour|

## Trivia
- The Hunting Enjoyer perk only affects [[Attributes|Shards]] obtained from [[Fishing]].
- The Happy Tree Friends perk is most likely a reference to the show Happy Tree Friends.
- The [[Attributes|Battle Frog]] ( [[Rana Shard]]) attribute buffs the pet's stats by up to +30 [[Strength]].
- The [[Attributes|Foraging Frog]] ( [[Treefrog Shard]]) attribute buffs the pet's stats by up to +10 [[Foraging Fortune]].

# Griffin Pet

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: GRIFFIN
Tooltip: Combat Pet; Strength: +0.5➡50; Crit Chance: +0.1➡10%; Crit Damage: +0.5➡50%; Attack Speed: +0.25➡25%; Odyssey 2 types of  Mythological can spawn from Griffin Burrows. Their stats scale with your Griffin's rarity.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
A Griffin Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]] that can be obtained from [[Diana]] while she is the [[Mayor Election|Mayor]] with the [[Mythological Ritual]] active. It increases the difficulty and rewards from the [[Mythological|Mythological Mobs]] based on its rarity.
## Obtaining
A [[Rarity|COMMON]] Griffin Pet can be purchased from [[Diana]] for 25,000 [[Coins]] when she is elected as [[Mayor Election|Mayor]] with the [[Mythological Ritual]] active. It can then be upgraded with [[Griffin Upgrade Stone|Griffin Upgrade Stones]] at [[Kat]] to higher rarities.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|100 [[Coins]]|1x Uncommon Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]])|1 Minute|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|1x Rare Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]])|1 Hour|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|1x Epic Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]])|1 Day|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]|1x Legendary Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]])|3 Days|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|1x Mythic Griffin Upgrade Stone ([[Griffin Upgrade Stone]])|1 Hour|
## Usage
### Mythological Ritual
While the Griffin Pet is active, [[Mythological]] creatures can be excavated from [[Griffin Burrows]]. The rarity of the Pet determines the strength and prefix of the creatures summoned, and you cannot damage [[Mythological]] mobs without having the rarity of the Griffin Pet required to summon it or higher equipped.
### [[Four-Eyed Fish]]
Players can equip their Griffin Pets with a [[Four-Eyed Fish]], which grants 13 [[Magic Find]] and 55 [[Defense]], as well as an additional 2,000 [[Coins]] when excavating [[Griffin Burrows]]: this is affected by [[Crown of Avarice]] and the [[Attributes|King of Greed]] attribute.

## Trivia
- It used to grant abilities at [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] and [[Rarity|EPIC]] instead of at [[Rarity|RARE]].
- King of Kings is likely a reference to the mythological roots of the Griffin; in Persia, the kings are referred to as Shahanshah, which means King of Kings.
- Prior to gaining a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity, the Griffin Pet used to be upgraded by clicking it with the [[Griffin Upgrade Stone]], similar to a [[Wisp Pet|Droplet Wisp Pet]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: GUARDIAN
Tooltip: Enchanting Pet; Defense: +0.5➡50; Intelligence: +1➡100; Lazerbeam Zaps your enemies for 0.02➡2x your  Intelligence every 3s.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Guardian Pet is an [[Enchanting]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
A [[Rarity|COMMON]]-[[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Guardian Pet can be obtained from the Superpairs minigame in the [[Experimentation Table]]. The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variant can also be selected in the [[Pity and RNG Meters|RNG Meter]], guaranteeing a drop at 150,000 XP.
A [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Guardian Pet can be obtained by giving a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Guardian Pet to [[Kat]] along with a [[Guardian Lucky Block]] and 1,000,000 [[Coins]] at level 1.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20k [[Coins]]||1 Hour|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]||6 Hours|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]||2 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|3M [[Coins]]|64x [[Enchanted Prismarine Shard]]|5 Days|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|1x [[Guardian Lucky Block]]|1 Hour|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Watcher Guardian Skin]]|[[Watcher Guardian Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - October 1, 2020 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Corrupt Eye Guardian Skin]]|[[Corrupt Eye Guardian Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Event Shop]] - Halloween 2024 - 150 Event Silver|
## Strategies
- The Guardian Pet's Lazerbeam ability can deal large amounts of damage when paired with armor that gives high [[Intelligence]], such as Very [[Wise Dragon Armor]] or [[Storm's Armor]].
  - Similarly, it is not recommended to use this pet with armor sets that give negative [[Intelligence]], such as [[Goblin Armor]] and [[Fairy Armor]].
- Most item abilities don't provoke the Guardian's Lazerbeam on the targets they hit.
  - The [[Voodoo Doll]] is an exception, thus pairing very well with the Guardian pet.
- Using the Guardian Pet whilst claiming rewards from the [[Experimentation Table]] is an effective way to gain both Pet EXP and [[Enchanting]] EXP.
## Trivia
- The Lazerbeam ability is disabled on Private Islands.
- Before the [[Colosseum]] was disabled, this pet was disabled within it because of a bug that allowed players to attack other players when the attacker or the attacked is in the safe zone.
- Ability damage does not provoke the Guardian's Lazerbeam on the targets they hit.
- The Guardian Pet used to be a Combat Pet but was then changed to the first-ever (and only) [[Enchanting]] Pet in the 0.10 Enchanting Update.
- If the player has negative [[Intelligence]], the ability deals zero damage instead of dealing negative damage or healing the target.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|4x [[Rusty Coin]]|1 Hour|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|16x [[Rusty Coin]]|2 Hours|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|200k [[Coins]]|8x [[Busted Belt Buckle]]|6 Hours|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|1x [[Old Leather Boot]]|2 Days|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|1x [[Scuttler Shell]]|1 Hour|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.

|-|-|-|-|

|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|2.5k [[Coins]]|1x [[Move Jerry]]|20 Seconds|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|1x [[Move Jerry]]|30 Seconds|

|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|25k [[Coins]]|1x [[Jerry 3D Glasses]]|60 Seconds|

## Trivia
- The Jerry Pet is one of the only pets that gives negative stats, along with the [[Rift Ferret Pet]].
- The Jerry Pet is also one of the few pets that has the exact same stats from [[Rarity|COMMON]] to [[Rarity|EPIC]].
- Prior to version 0.18.3, the Jerry Pet had to hold the [[Jerry 3D Glasses]] to be [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity.
  - Any Jerry Pets still holding the item when the update released were instantly upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] and had their Jerry 3D Glasses removed.

# Mithril Golem Pet
Aliases: Common Mithril Golem Pet, Mithril Golem, Mithril Pet, Mithril pet, Mystery Mithril Golem Pet, Mythril Golem
Categories: Mythic pets, Pets, Craftable items, Templates with deprecated argument names, Mithril Collection, Dwarven Mines, Mining pets
[Mithril Golem Pet]
Type: [[Mining]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Mithril III (3) ([[Mithril]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Mithril]] 144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) [[Egg]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: MITHRIL_GOLEM
Tooltip: Mining Pet; True Defense: +0.5➡50; Mining Fortune: +0.25➡25; Mithril Affinity Grants +1➡100 Mining Speed when mining Mithril.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Mithril Golem Pet is a [[Mining]] [[Pets|Pet]] unlocked at [[Mithril|Mithril III (3)]]. The crafting recipe requires 256 (4 stacks) [[Mithril]] and an [[Enchanted Egg]]. The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variant can be upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] by [[Kat]] in exchange for 100,000 [[Coins]] and a [[Pure Mithril Gem]].
The Mithril Golem Pet is good for those looking to spend a lot of time in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. Its abilities provide a boost to [[Mining Speed]] while mining [[Mithril]], increases ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] gained from all sources, and gives increased [[Combat]] stats and [[Magic Find]] to players on Mining Islands for those looking to fight the monsters.
## Obtaining

|Mithril III (3) ([[Mithril]])|1x [[Enchanted Egg]] 256x [[Mithril]]|Recipe: [[Mithril]] x32 ×8 + [[Enchanted Egg]] → [Lvl 1➡100] Mithril Golem|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Mithril]]|5 Minutes|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Mithril]]|10 Minutes|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|25k [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Mithril]]|15 Minutes|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|32x [[Enchanted Mithril]]|30 Minutes|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]|64x [[Enchanted Mithril]] 1x [[Pure Mithril Gem]]|1 Hour|

## Trivia
- The Subterranean Battler increases all [[Combat]] stats on [[Mining Islands]].
  - Affected [[Combat]] stats include:
    - [[Health]]
    - [[Defense]]
    - [[True Defense]]
    - [[Strength]]
    - [[Crit Chance]]
    - [[Crit Damage]]
    - [[Attack Speed]]
    - [[Ferocity]]
    - [[Swing Range]]
    - [[Intelligence]]
    - [[Ability Damage]]
    - [[Health Regen]]
    - [[Vitality]]
    - [[Mending]]
  - Mining Islands include:
    - [[Gold Mine]]
    - [[Deep Caverns]]
    - [[Gunpowder Mines]]
    - [[Lapis Quarry]]
    - [[Pigmen's Den]]
    - [[Slimehill]]
    - [[Diamond Reserve]]
    - [[Obsidian Sanctuary]]
    - [[Dwarven Mines]]
    - [[Crystal Hollows]]
    - [[Glacite Tunnels]]
    - [[Glacite Mineshafts]]
- The Mithril Golem Pet displays an increase of +20% for all [[Combat]] stats, however the actual damage increase is much higher, due to it boosting both [[Strength]] and [[Crit Damage]].
- The Mithril Golem Pet gives the most [[True Defense]] out of any pet in the game.
- Despite The Smell Of Powder stating it boosts all sources, it does not boost the ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] from [[Commissions]].
- This Pet's model resembles the helmets of [[Star Sentry|Star Sentries]], albeit recolored.

# Rabbit Pet
Aliases: Mystery Rabbit Pet
Categories: Mythic pets, Pets, Craftable items, Templates with deprecated argument names, Raw Rabbit Collection, Farming pets
[Rabbit Pet]
Type: [[Farming]] Pet ([[Pets]])
Rarities: COMMON ([[Rarity]])–MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Collection ([[Collections]]): Raw Rabbit III (3) ([[Raw Rabbit]])
Mob: Floating Head
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Raw Rabbit]] 144x (2 stacks plus 16 (144 in total)) [[Egg]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: RABBIT
Tooltip: Farming Pet; Health: +1➡100; Speed: +0.2➡20; Happy Feet Jump potions also give +0.3➡30; speed.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; COMMON
The Rabbit Pet is a craftable [[Farming]] [[Pets|Pet]] unlocked at [[Raw Rabbit|Raw Rabbit III (3)]]. Crafting requires 256 (4 stacks) [[Raw Rabbit]] and an [[Enchanted Egg]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Raw Rabbit III (3) ([[Raw Rabbit]])|1x [[Enchanted Egg]] 256x [[Raw Rabbit]]|Recipe: [[Raw Rabbit]] x32 ×8 + [[Enchanted Egg]] → [Lvl 1➡100] Rabbit|
The Rabbit Pet can be upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] via [[Kat]] for 100,000 [[Coins]], 64 (1 stack) [[Enchanted Raw Rabbit]], 32 [[Enchanted Rabbit Hide]], 32 [[Enchanted Rabbit Foot]] and 1 [[Chocolate Syringe]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|5k [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Raw Rabbit]] 256x [[Rabbit Hide]] 256x [[Rabbit's Foot]]|5 Minutes|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Raw Rabbit]] 4x [[Enchanted Rabbit Hide]] 4x [[Enchanted Rabbit Foot]]|10 Minutes|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|25k [[Coins]]|16x [[Enchanted Raw Rabbit]] 8x [[Enchanted Rabbit Hide]] 8x [[Enchanted Rabbit Foot]]|15 Minutes|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]|32x [[Enchanted Raw Rabbit]] 16x [[Enchanted Rabbit Hide]] 16x [[Enchanted Rabbit Foot]]|30 Minutes|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]|64x [[Enchanted Raw Rabbit]] 32x [[Enchanted Rabbit Hide]] 32x [[Enchanted Rabbit Foot]] 1x [[Chocolate Syringe]]|1 Hour|

## Trivia
- The Efficient Farming ability does not affect farming minions that spawn mobs, e.g. [[Pig Minion|Pig Minions]].
- The Happy Feet ability works with the [[Rabbit Hat]].
- There is a bug where the Happy Feet ability will always be maxed out regardless of pet level.
- [[Rabbit Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Rabbit Pets can boost other people's [[Minions]] as a guest.
- If two or more people on the same [[Private Island]] have [[Rabbit Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Rabbit Pets, the person who has been there the longest will be the one who boosts the minions.
- The Rabbit Pet is recommended if you are farming for exp, as it gives +30 [[Farming Wisdom]] when at level 100 and at least [[Rarity|EPIC]] rarity.

# Rat Pet
Categories: Mythic pets, Pets, Mob Drops, Templates with deprecated argument names, The Uprising
[Rat Pet]
Type: [[Combat]] Morph ([[Morphs]])
Rarities: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])–MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
Mob: Floating Head On Floor
Obtained: Rare drop from Rats ([[Rat]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Rideable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET
Pet ID: RAT
Tooltip: Combat Morph; Health: +1.25➡125; Strength: +0.6➡60; Crit Damage: +0.25➡25%; Morph Right-click your summoned pet to morph into it!; CHEESE!; As a Rat, you smell CHEESE nearby! Yummy!; Rat's Blessing Has a chance to grant a random player +2.05➡7 Magic Find for 20.4➡60 seconds after finding a yummy piece of Cheese! If the player gets a drop during this buff, you have a 20% chance to get it too.; Right-click to add this pet to your pet menu!; LEGENDARY
The Rat Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Morphs|Morph]] that was added after [[Resistance Movement|The Uprising]] as a perk of 50[[Ranks|[MINISTER]]] 56ms: "Infestation". It was the first [[Morphs|Morph]] ever created in the game.
## Obtaining
The Rat Pet can be obtained by killing [[Rat (Hub)]] found in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] or by killing [[Rat|Rat Pests]] found in [[The Garden]].
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv3]|[[Rat (Hub)]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.2%|
|[Lv1]|[[Rat]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.4%* ([[Pests]])|
A [[Rat Pet]] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Rat Pet can be upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity by giving [[Kat]] a [[Rat Jetpack]] and 1,000,000 [[Coins]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|1x [[Rat Jetpack]]|1 hour|
## Usage
The Rat Pet grants its owner the ability to disguise themselves as a Rat while being able to control its movements. This ability functions similarly to the [[Rock Pet|Rock Pet's]] "Rideable" ability.
The Rat Pet is controlled by facing the direction the player wants to move and selecting a hotbar slot (1 being the fastest and 9, the slot with the [[SkyBlock Menu]], being the slowest, causing the player to remain stationary. Slot 8 is the hotbar slot with the slowest speed where the player is still moving.)
When riding the Rat Pet, a message will sometimes appear notifying the player that there is [[Tasty Cheese]] nearby. When collecting nearby cheese, the Rat Pet will grant a random player [[Magic Find]]. The amount of [[Magic Find]] and time the buff is granted depends on the level of the Rat Pet. While a player is given this boost, the owner of the pet has a 20% chance to receive most drops the buffed player gets. This does not apply to [[Slayer|slayer bosses]] and [[Primal Fear]]. It also does not grant progress towards [[Collections]].
When collecting [[Tasty Cheese]], the player cannot buff another player who already has a Rat's Blessing active. If there is no player without a buff active in the lobby, then no one will receive the buff.

## Trivia
- This pet was added as part of 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] 56ms's mayor perk, due to his position as a [[Resistance General]].
- Entering the portal in the Hub while riding the Rat Pet won't teleport the player.
- The Rat Pet, unlike the [[Horse Pet]] and [[Skeleton Horse Pet]], will push the player through non-solid blocks (slabs, fences, etc.). This will cause the player to suffocate.
  - Like the [[Horse Pet]] and [[Skeleton Horse Pet]], the player can still glitch through walls as long as they are one block thick.
- If the player is the only person in the lobby when collecting a [[Tasty Cheese]], then no one will receive the buff.
- The Rat Pet is currently the only [[Morphs|Morph]].
- The CHEESE! ability (and consequently the Rat's Blessing ability) will not activate on a Player's [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]], making player unable to build farms that allow them to AFK and get loads of cheese efficiently.
- While riding the Rat Pet, the player is immune to fall damage.
- When the [[Great Spook Staff]]'s Spook ability is used while riding the Rat Pet, they are visibly riding the rat after the ability runs out.
- If a player is morphed into a Rat Pet and moves near a player with a [[Crow Pet]] equipped, the Rat Pet is despawned and the following message is sent in chat:
OOPS! Your Crow ate <player>'s Rat!

Aliases: Snowman

The Snowman Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Snowman Pet can be obtained by opening [[Red Gift|Red Gifts]] from the [[Season of Jerry]] event. There is a 0.05% (1 in 1938.24 gifts) chance of receiving a Snowman Pet from the Red Gifts.
A Snowman Pet can be upgraded to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity by giving [[Kat]] a [[Magic Top Hat]] and 1,000,000 [[Coins]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.

|-|-|-|-|

## Trivia
- The Snowman Pet's Snow Cannon ability can hit mobs up to 8 blocks away. It is affected [[Ferocity]] and is affected by the damage bonus of One For All.
- The Snowman Pet's Snow Cannon ability is able to damage [[Defend Jerry's Workshop|Liquid Hot Magmas]] and the [[Defend Jerry's Workshop|Magma Cubes]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]]. If the [[Defend Jerry's Workshop|Strongarm]] power-up is active, the snowball it fires deals double damage as well.
- [[Rarity|COMMON]] Snowman Pets were once obtainable, but no longer are. One of the last remaining ones can be found on 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] TimeDeo's island.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|5M [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Tarantula Web]]|10 Days|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|1x [[Fake Neuroscience Degree]]|1 Hour|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Greenbottle Tarantula Skin]]|[[Greenbottle Tarantula Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - March 23, 2024 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Pink Tarantula Skin]]|[[Pink Tarantula Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - March 23, 2024 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Tips
- Despite the Tarantula Pet no longer granting a multiplicative [[Combat|Combat XP]] buff against Spiders, it still grants more additive [[Combat Wisdom]] than the [[Wolf Pet]] against Spiders.
## Trivia
- The Tarantula Pet is the only pet in the game where its [[Rarity|EPIC]] and [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] variants can be obtained from two completely different sources.
  - The [[Magma Cube Pet]] is similar, but differs in rarity.
- The Tarantula Pet is one of 5 pets designed for its respective [[Slayer]]: the others being the [[Ghoul Pet]] for [[Zombie Slayer]], the [[Hound Pet]] for [[Wolf Slayer]], the [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Enderman Pet]] for [[Enderman Slayer]], and the [[Wisp Pet]] for [[Blaze Slayer]].
- If the player already has an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Tarantula Pet in their pets menu when they obtain a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] one from [[Arachne]], their existing pet's rarity will be upgraded. This is a unique mechanic that so far isn't used for any other pet drops.
- The Tarantula Pet uses the Blood Spider player head texture.
- The [[Attributes|Battle Arachnid]] ( [[Areita Shard]]) attribute buffs the pet's stats by up to +20 [[Crit Damage]].
- The [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Leggings]] and [[Primordial Armor|Primordial Leggings]] buff the pet's stats by +50% and +100% respectively.

[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

|Jul 15, 2026|Changed Webbed Cells's anti-healing reduction to now only target the [[Tarantula Broodfather]]'s [[Vitality]] reduction.|
|Sep 21, 2026|Arachnid Slayer now grants +50 [[Combat Wisdom]] instead of 1.5x Combat XP.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Jul 9, 2024|0.20.4|Fixed the [[Strength]] and [[Magic Find]] displayed in the [[Tarantula Pet]] MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) Tarantula Pet's lore not scaling with the pet's level.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Changed Webbed Cells's anti-healing reduction to now only target the [[Tarantula Broodfather]]'s [[Vitality]] reduction.|

# "Hunter" Tobias
Aliases: Hunter Tobias, Tobias
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
["Hunter" Tobias]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Introduces the player to the [[Critterdex]].
Location: [[Critter Safari]]
Coordinates: -49.5 66 2.5
"Hunter" Tobias is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Critter Safari]]. They introduce the player to the [[Critterdex]].
## Interface
- This article is a work in progress.
- This article is incomplete and will be expanded on over the course of the article's creation.
- If you see missing, incorrect or out of date information, you are welcomed to edit this page.
This article was last edited by Mrxp (contribs|logs|block) on 7:43, October 2 2026 (24 days ago)
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: What's up? [NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: I'm the greatest hunter around - the name's Tobias. [NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: I'm actually so great that I've caught every single Critter in the Critter Safari. [NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: ...And, before you ask, my success has nothing to do with the fact that my dad owns the place. Select an option: [Nice!] [Suuuuuure...]|
|Selecting [Nice!]|[NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: Anyways, why don't we see how many Critters you've caught?. [NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: ...Less than I have, for sure.|
|Selecting [Suuuuuure...]|[NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: ... [NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: What did you say to me? [NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: Say sorry right now, or I won't let you use the Critterdex.|
|Before saying "sorry" in chat|[NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: I'm not letting you use the Critterdex until you say I am very sorry Tobias and regret my actions deeply. [NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: ... [NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: I'm waiting!|
|Upon saying "sorry" in chat|[NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: That's what I thought! Thanks for acknowledging your inferiority. [NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: Now, here's the Critterdex. [NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: You can use it to view all of the Critters you've caught while in the Critter Safari. [NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: While you'll never catch as many as me, you should try your best anyways.|
|After activating the 7 bells|[NPC] "Hunter" Tobias: Uh guys, you better come and see this...|
## Trivia
- "Hunter" Tobias is implied to be a fraud or a "nepo baby", with [[Helia]]'s [[Chapters]] suggesting that the first person to complete the [[Critterdex]] was a nepo baby.
  - This is further implied through the usage of quotes in his name, as well as him denying that his success is correlated to his father's ownership of the [[Critter Safari]].

# Aatrox
Aliases: Aatrox the Maddox's Brother-in-Law, Atrox, Candidate Aatrox, Mayor Aatrox, Minister Aatrox
Categories: Non-player characters, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
[Aatrox Aatrox]
Also known as: Mayor Aatrox
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
[Locations]
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]), or nearby when elected as Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6.5 79 19.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor or Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: -3.5 49 34.5
Tooltip: Year # Candidate; Votes: # (#%); Last elected: #y ago; __________________________ SLASHED Pricing Starting slayer quests is half price.; Slayer XP Buff Earn 25% more Slayer XP.; Pathfinder Gain rare drops 20% more often.; __________________________; ✯ Minister Perks are also granted if this mayor wins second place!; Click to vote for Aatrox!
Aatrox, Maddox's Brother-In-Law, is a mayoral candidate that can be elected during the [[Mayor Election]] event. His perks focus around [[Slayer]].
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Aatrox may run for election with up to 3 possible perks.
  [[Aatrox]] Aatrox Maddox's Brother-in-Law
1. SLASHED Pricing: Starting [[Slayer]] quests is half price.
2. Slayer XP Buff: Earn 25% more [[Slayer]] XP.
3. Pathfinder: Gain rare drops 20% more often.
## Mechanics
Pathfinder increases the chance of obtaining drops affected by [[Magic Find]] from [[Slayer]] Bosses by 20% (1.2x). This is done by increasing [[Magic Find]] against Slayer Bosses using the following formula:
NewMagicFind=(OldMagicFind×1.2)+20
## Dialogue
- The color of the NPC's name changes depending on which color they are in the current [[Mayor Election]]. Yellow has been used as a default color.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aatrox: Me Aatrox. [NPC] Aatrox: Maddox older brother. [NPC] Aatrox: Maddox is brain. Me slay. If you vote me, you slay too!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Mayor Aatrox: Thank for voting me mayor. Me need food.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Mayor Aatrox: You slay.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Mayor Aatrox: Me find you.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aatrox: Why talk? Just slay.|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aatrox: Maddox always hiding. Me know why.|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Aatrox: If me lose mayor, me find you.|
## Trivia
- In the original patch notes for the 0.9 update, Aatrox is referred to as [[Maddox the Slayer]]'s brother-in-law, but his dialogue introduces Aatrox as Maddox's younger brother.
  - [[Elle]] refers to Aatrox as Maddox's brother in the Mage branch of the Crimson Isle questline.
- His name is a reference to a League of Legends champion of the same name.
  - Alternatively, it can be directly based on the Latin word atrōx meaning horrible, cruel, terrible or violent.
- Aatrox was the original name of Maddox before it was changed. This is hinted on the ID name of the [[Maddox Batphone]]: AATROX_BATPHONE
  - In the Mage branch of the Crimson Isle questline, Elle explains that Aatrox has been crafting simple Ability Phones for Maddox's use. This serves as an explanation for the ID having Aatrox's name.
- Aatrox's bonus to Slayer XP is multiplicative with the one from [[Raffle of the Century|Century Celebration]].
- On June 3, 2024, when what would become 0.20.3 — Better Mayors update first made it on [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], the following has been said: "You didn't hear this from me but apparently Aatrox and Paul failed to hand in their new campaign plans in enough time. Aatrox claimed he couldn't read Clerk Seraphine's memo (even though she sent out multiple, well in advance), (...)."
  - No news about their 4th perks have been revealed since.

Adventurer Saul is an [[NPC]] in [[Dungeon Hub]] that allows players to queue into dungeons alone, after they have beaten [[The Professor]], by giving them [[Saul's Recommendation]].

|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|

[NPC] Adventurer Saul: Not the mention that The Catacombs is extremenly dangerous to explore alone. |Without having beaten [[The Professor]]|[NPC] Adventurer Saul: Unfortunately, it seems you haven't yet defeated The Professor! Come back when you have done that!|
|Having beaten [[The Professor]]|[NPC] Adventurer Saul: So, have you defeated The Professor yet? Select an option: [Uh, yeah. I'm the best.]|
|After selecting [Uh, yeah. I'm the best.]|[NPC] Adventurer Saul: Then take this letter to Mort whenever you feel like entering The Catacombs alone. Make sure that you're not in a party. [NPC] Adventurer Saul: Best of luck. You claimed a Saul's Recommendation!|
|After claiming [[Saul's Recommendation]]|[NPC] Adventurer Saul: Good luck on your solo adventurers. I look forward to hearing about your triumphs within The Catacombs! Select an option: [I need a new recommendation letter.]|
|After selecting [I need a new recommendation letter.] without having a [[Saul's Recommendation]] in inventory|[NPC] Adventurer Saul: ...|
|After selecting [I need a new recommendation letter.] while having a [[Saul's Recommendation]] in inventory|[NPC] Adventurer Saul: ... [NPC] Adventurer Saul: I can see that you already have my recommendation letter on you!|
|After selecting [I need a new recommendation letter.] after handing in the letter to [[Mort]]|[NPC] Adventurer Saul: ... [NPC] Adventurer Saul: You've already handed in my recommendation letter! You don't need another one.|

Alatar is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Alatar: Time flows differently in this dimension. [NPC] Alatar: A lot of wizards, including me, try and study Time around here. [NPC] Alatar: Tomorrow you might come back here yesterday. [NPC] Alatar: Maybe we already had this conversation in the future. [NPC] Alatar: Is there even a before and an after in this place? ## Lore
[[Dr. Emmett]] had Alatar summon an exact copy of a letter that [[The Watcher|Lathrop]] had failed to deliver. This and [[Udium]]'s dialogue imply that Alatar doesn't originate in Rift Dimension, although it is unknown how he got in.
## Trivia
- Alatar is one of a few NPCs who are not originally from [[Rift Dimension]], alongside [[Julian]], [[Barry]], [[Dr. Emmett]] and [[Tel Kar]].

# Alchemage
Aliases: Alchmage
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Alchemage]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Gives the player [[The Alchemist's Staff]] and 250 Reputation ([[Factions]]) after giving various items.
Location: [[Scarleton Bank]]
Coordinates: -86 118 -771
Alchemage is an [[NPC]] found above the [[Scarleton Bank]]. They ask for various gold-themed items in exchange for [[The Alchemist's Staff]] and 250 [[Factions|Reputation]].
## Quest
The player needs to give Alchemage these resources in the following order:
  64x [[Gold Ingot]] 5x [[Golden Plate]] 1x [[Diver's Armor|Diver's Mask]] 1x [[Diver's Armor|Diver's Trunks]] 32x [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 5x [[Golden Powder]] 32x [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]] 1x [[Golden Ball]]
This costs roughly from the [[Bazaar]], alongside a [[Snorkeling Armor|Snorkeling Visor]] and [[Snorkeling Armor|Snorkeling Pants]]. After giving the [[Golden Ball]], the player will receive [[The Alchemist's Staff]] and 250 [[Factions|Reputation]] for their current [[Factions|Faction]].
This quest is shared with [[Baar]], meaning that any progress contributed to one of the two [[NPC|NPCs]] remains on the other.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Not in a Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Alchemage: Hello there, adventurer! [NPC] Alchemage: You look like someone who could help me with my research! [NPC] Alchemage: If you join our faction I might have some tasks for you. [NPC] Alchemage: I'll make it worth your while.|
|In the Barbarian Faction ([[Factions]]) without the quest already started|[NPC] Alchemage: You have no business talking to me.|
|Starting the quest|[NPC] Alchemage: Hello there, adventurer! [NPC] Alchemage: I'm the local alchemist, I do research on different types of gold. [NPC] Alchemage: If you have some time maybe you could help me with some tasks. [NPC] Alchemage: I'll make sure it's worth your time. [NPC] Alchemage: Talk to me again if you want to help out.|
|Needing 64 (1 stack) [[Gold Ingot]]|[NPC] Alchemage: To start out I'll need some generic gold to experiment on, could you get me a stack?|
|Needing 5 [[Golden Plate]]|[NPC] Alchemage: Next I need some flat gold to test how reflective gold is, could you try forging gold into 5 gold plates?|
|Needing 1 Diver's Mask ([[Diver's Armor]])|[NPC] Alchemage: I heard there exists a golden helmet that helps you swim faster in water. I would like to test its magical properties, could you bring me one?|
|Needing 1 Diver's Trunks ([[Diver's Armor]])|[NPC] Alchemage: It seems like the first piece you brought me is only 25% of the magical power, could you get me the leggings?|
|Needing 32 [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|[NPC] Alchemage: Now I need a lot of compacted gold, it has to be extremely dense. A half stack should do.|
|Needing 5 [[Golden Powder]]|[NPC] Alchemage: There is a fine-grained gold substance somewhere in the Hub, I'll need 5 of that.|
|Needing 32 [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]]|[NPC] Alchemage: Next I'm going to need a vegetable that is made out of solid gold. I want to experiment with how gold interacts with organics, maybe you could find some, like a half stack?|
|Needing 1 [[Golden Ball]]|[NPC] Alchemage: I just need one last thing, there's an extremely dangerous scientist who sells an assortment of items, he has a special rounded type of gold. Try to convince him to sell you it.|
|Giving 1 [[Golden Ball]]|[NPC] Alchemage: Finally, my research is complete! [NPC] Alchemage: You have single-handedly sped up my research by several years. [NPC] Alchemage: As promised, here is your reward.|
|After completing the quest|[NPC] Alchemage: If I ever need your help, I will be sure to let you know!|
|Wrong amount of an item given|[NPC] Alchemage: This isn't the correct amount of the item I asked for. [NPC] Alchemage: I need the EXACT amount I asked for!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Alchemage: I miscalculated, I need more for my research.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Alchemage: As promised, here is your reward.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Alchemage: You have single-handedly slowed down my research by several years.|
## Trivia
- Its name is a portmanteau of the words alchemy and mage.

# Apprentice (Crimson Isle)
Categories: Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Apprentice]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Appears during the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]).
Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
Coordinates: -521 104.187 -917
[Metadata]
ID: SLEEPING_APPRENTICE
The Apprentice is an [[NPC]] that appears briefly in the [[Crimson Isle]] during the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]].
## Quest
In the middle of the [[Factions|Mage Faction]]'s [[Crimson Isle Main Questline|Questline]], [[Dean]] tasks the player with finding the apprentice, who was last seen by the [[Burning Desert]]. There, the player finds [[Captain Ahone]], who also tasks the player with finding the Apprentice, and gives out their [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] contact so they can be called when they are found.
The Apprentice can then be found just north of [[The Dukedom]], lying on the floor. After calling [[Captain Ahone]] about it, he, [[Krevius]], and [[Grelius]] show up, splash him with healing potions, and then all four [[NPC|NPCs]] disappear. The player is then tasked with going back to [[Dean]] with this information.

# Arba
Categories: Non-player characters, Crimson Isle
[Arba]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Talks about time travel, which hints towards the conditions for finding the [[Kuudra Loremaster]].
Location: [[Scarleton]]
Coordinates: -3.5 93 -820.5
Arba is an [[NPC]] located in [[Scarleton]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Arba: This is absurd! [NPC] Arba: Everyone knows time travel is a myth! [NPC] Arba: Excuse me, can't you see we are trying to have a conversation here?|
|None|[NPC] Arba: Please leave us alone!|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Mage 1/4)|[NPC] Arba: Oh, a new face! Welcome to the City of Magic! [NPC] Arba: We used to be Pig Farmers centuries ago, but thanks to Sulphur we are now superior beings capable of mastering the magical elements! [NPC] Arba: If you are interested in tourist attractions I suggest you visit our Cathedral, which is also our Academy now.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); Less than 1,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Arba: [amount] Intelligence! That's...ok I guess!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); 1,000-2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Arba: [amount] Intelligence! Wow that's not bad at all!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 2/4); More than 2,000 [[Intelligence]]|[NPC] Arba: [amount] Intelligence! What, how? Who are you even?|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 3/4)|[NPC] Arba: Have you tried the new ability phone? [NPC] Arba: The new one is barely better than the one from last year, people still buy them like sheep... [NPC] Arba: Wait, you don't even have an Abiphone? [NPC] Arba: Yikes...|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Mage 4/4)|[NPC] Arba: Ah, the Piglins yes, quite troublesome they are. [NPC] Arba: I wouldn't go there if I were you.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Arba: You seem suspicious.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Arba: Sorry, I can't talk to you right now.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Mages are spoken to|[NPC] Arba: Please stop bothering me.|
## Trivia
- Arba's name is the "Abra" backwards, a word associated with the phrase "Abracadabra Alakazam". It is often used in magic.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Mar 8, 2022|Added Arba.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Arba.|

# Arch
Categories: Non-player characters, Article stubs, Crimson Isle
[Arch]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Rewards the player with the Kuudra Follower Chestplate ([[Kuudra Follower Armor]]) under certain conditions.
Location: [[Stronghold]]
Coordinates: -360.5 189 -552.5
Arch is an [[NPC]] located on top of the [[Stronghold]]. They appear after defeating [[Kuudra]] once.
## Quest
If the following conditions are met, and the player interacts with Arch, they will give the player a [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Kuudra Follower Chestplate]]:
6 helpers are required, and must be wearing full [[Ember Armor]] or [[Rekindled Ember Armor]] and have a [[Kuudra Pet]] equipped that is [Lv81] or higher. They must be in the following locations:
- 2 in the [[Stronghold]] (the [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Chestplate]] can also be claimed by these players)
- 2 in the [[Dragontail Townsquare]]
- 2 in the [[Scarleton Plaza]]
The helpers' current factions are irrelevant. Players to claim the [[Kuudra Follower Armor|Chestplate]] have no additional requirements.
## Dialogue
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Arch: Hello traveler! [NPC] Arch: Kuudra will return one day but we must first put aside our differences and be ready. [NPC] Arch: We must show Kuudra that we stand united for its return.|
|Wearing Ember ([[Ember Armor]]) or [[Rekindled Ember Armor]]|[NPC] Arch: Hello traveler! [NPC] Arch: That's a very impressive armor set you are wearing, Kuudra would be impressed! [NPC] Arch: Kuudra will return one day but we must first put aside our differences and be ready. [NPC] Arch: We must show Kuudra that we stand united for its return.|
|Claiming Kuudra Follower Chestplate ([[Kuudra Follower Armor]])|[NPC] Arch: We are united! Kuudra SHOW YOURSELF TO US. [NPC] Arch: ... [NPC] Arch: ... [NPC] Arch: ... [NPC] Arch: Why didn't it show itself... [NPC] Arch: Maybe it won't ever return... [NPC] Arch: Thank you for your dedication adventurer, here is a token of my gratitude.|
|Showing Unpleasant Kuudra Teeth Plaque ([[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]]) without a [[Kuudra Pet]] equipped|[NPC] Arch: I see you holding something there... [NPC] Arch: And it seems as though you need some assistance. [NPC] Arch: I would be able to provide it, but you came without the correct friend.|
|Showing Unpleasant Kuudra Teeth Plaque ([[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]]) with a [[Kuudra Pet]] equipped|[NPC] Arch: Ah yes! You are here with the right companion. [NPC] Arch: It seems as though I can now properly assist you. [NPC] Arch: Let me go ahead and help you break down that item. [NPC] Arch: I wish you luck on your journey, adventurer!|

# Archaeologist
Aliases: Archeologist
Categories: Non-player characters
[Archaeologist]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Relic Hunting ([[Quests]])
Description: Introduces the player to [[Relics]].
Location: [[Archaeologist's Camp]]
Coordinates: -360.5 111 -290
The Archaeologist is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Archaeologist's Camp]], on the [[Spider's Den]]. They give the player the [[Archaeologist's Compass]] and ask them to find 28 hidden [[Relics]] spread across the [[Spider's Den]], rewarding them 10,000 [[Coins]] for each and an additional 30,000 [[Coins]] after finding them all.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Archaeologist: Hey, are you an adventurer? [NPC] Archaeologist: There are Relics I am searching for all around this island! [NPC] Archaeologist: But I am not strong enough to find them. [NPC] Archaeologist: Take my compass to assist you in your journey!|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Some spider eggs look a bit different, check behind those.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Are you sure you looked through every cobweb?|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Down there, multiple rooms are blocked by cobwebs.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: The spiders are hiding some of the relics behind cobwebs.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Look around spider eggs too, and cobwebs!|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Dig through gravel and you should be able to find a relic.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: I'm fairly sure there is a Relic nearby the dragon's tail.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Don't forget to look down those caves, there are plenty down there lying around.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: I managed to find one behind a spider egg, but once again I just couldn't reach it...|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: When in doubt, check behind cobwebs!|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Look up around the caves, some spiders have their nest behind some cobwebs up there.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: I won't lie, some of those I could find myself, but I hate cobwebs.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Spiders often dig up mushrooms and create tiny rooms below, make sure to check those!|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: There might be some below mushrooms too!|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: One of the Relics is inside a tiny hole inside the caves.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: There are some relics up there hidden on the Spider's Den.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Always check the roof in the caves, there are holes behind the cobwebs.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: You know that breakable wall in the caves? Look around it.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Walk around the edges, you will probably find some cave entrances.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: I know a spot down in the caves but I just have no idea how to get into that room, the entrance is tiny.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: There is a relic a little below a Fairy Soul in the caves.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Make sure you check all the mushrooms, sometimes there are tiny holes behind them.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Sometimes look behind leaves too, there are many hidden caves on this island!|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: I believe there is one down in the spider cave, behind a wall of cobwebs.|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: I heard rumors that there is a man-made hole inside the cave, with a Fairy Soul even!|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Are you sure you checked every mushroom?|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: I found one below the house too but I really can't access it, I'm not sure how to get there...|
|Before Finding all [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Pretty sure there is one nearby the Dragon skull.|
|After Finding All [[Relics]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: Wow! You found all the Relics. [NPC] Archaeologist: Take this as a reward, you've earned it.|
|After Finding All [[Relics]] and [[Fairy Souls]]|[NPC] Archaeologist: I found an interesting specimen in the spider caves, perhaps there's other stuff to find in there.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Archaeologist: Oh, hello there! You must be the adventurer who helped who helped me find all those relics.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Archaeologist: I came to visit your Garden and see if I could find any artifacts. I heard there might be something interesting here.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Archaeologist: Oh, that's alright. I understand you're busy with your own adventures.|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Archaeologist as a Garden Visitor ([[The Garden]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Mar 9, 2021|0.11.2|Added Archaeologist.|

# Argofay Bughunter
Aliases: Bughunter
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Argofay Bughunter]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Rewards the player with the [[Silkwire Stick]] and [[Silkrider Safety Belt]] in exchange for various items.
Location: [[Wyld Woods]]
Coordinates: -85 108 97
The Argofay Bughunter is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wyld Woods]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Interface
Once the player gives the Argofay Bughunter the requested 6 [[Larva Silk]], the player will be rewarded with a [[Silkwire Stick]], opening the following menu.
Upon completing the Argofay Bughunter's quest and giving them 8 [[Bottled Odonata]], the player will be rewarded with the [[Silkrider Safety Belt]], opening the following menu.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: We don't eat much. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Sometimes we eat Odonatas. Select an option: [Hi?]|
|Selecting [Hi?]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Yes. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Hi. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: To collect Odonatas, you need to fly. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Use the larvas to fly. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Talk to Bugshopper, then show me a Larva Hook.|
|Without a [[Larva Hook]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Get a Larva Hook from Bugshopper.|
|With a [[Larva Hook]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Now use the hook. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Find a larva in the branches. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Hook it until it relinquishes its SILK. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Then come back.|
|Before bringing [[Larva Silk]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Find a larva in the branches. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Hook it until it relinquishes its silk. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Then come back.|
|With [[Larva Silk]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Good. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Do not eat the silk. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Otherwise you cannot fly. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Now go and bring back 6 Larva Silk.|
|Before bringing 6 [[Larva Silk]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Bring me 6 Larva Silk.|
|Bringing 6 [[Larva Silk]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Great. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: You may have noticed... [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: You can use silk to create wires. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Silkwire. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Use Silkwire to fly. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: With this stick.|
|After claiming [[Silkwire Stick]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Now talk to Bugshopper and show me an Odonata Bottle.|
|[[Silkwire Stick]] not in the player's inventory|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Lost stick?|
|Bringing an [[Empty Odonata Bottle]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Now is flying time. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Spot an Odonata out there. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: They are very small. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Lay out a Silkwire which crosses the Odonata. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Use the Silkwire Stick on the Silkwire to fly. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Then collect the Odonata using the Empty Odonata Bottle. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: I am hungry. Please bring back the Bottled Odonata.|
|Before bringing a [[Bottled Odonata]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Spot an Odonata out there. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Lay out a Silkwire which crosses it. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Use the Silkwire Stick on the Silkwire to fly. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Then collect the Odonata using the Empty Odonata Bottle. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Please bring a Bottled Odonata.|
|Bringing a [[Bottled Odonata]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Very very good. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Very tasty. Select an option: [Why do you say so few words?]|
|Selecting [Why do you say so few words?]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Because few words do trick. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: I taught you how to capture Odonatas. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Now please bring back 8 Bottled Odonata. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: I am very hungry. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: There is something in it for you if you do.|
|Before bringing 8 [[Bottled Odonata]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Please bring me 8 Bottled Odonata. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: There is something in it for you if you do.|
|Bringing 8 [[Bottled Odonata]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: So good. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: You are the best. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Take this belt. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: It's safer with it.|
|[[Silkrider Safety Belt]] not in the player's inventory|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Lost your belt? [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: That's unsafe.|
|After claiming [[Silkrider Safety Belt]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Thank you for the Odonatas. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: I am not hungry anymore. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Come back when I am hungry again.|
|Without 3x more [[Bottled Odonata]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: I am hungry. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Please bring me 3 Bottled Odonata. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: I will give you 5,000 Motes if you do.|
|Bringing 3x more [[Bottled Odonata]]|[NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Thank you. [NPC] Argofay Bughunter: Here are the Motes.|
## Trivia
- "Argofay" is likely Pig Latin for "Fargo".
- The line "Because few words do trick" is a reference to the character Kevin Malone from this scene in an episode of the NBC show The Office.

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Trying to pick up or modify the nearby [[Wheat Minion]]|[NPC] Arthur: Sorry, but that's mine!|

|None|[NPC] Arthur: If you place your Minions poorly, they will complain! [NPC] Arthur: Open their Menu and look for their Perfect Layout!|

|None|[NPC] Arthur: Use a Budget Hopper to make your Minions automatically sell their items once full!|
|None|[NPC] Arthur: My favorite Minion Upgrade is the Compactor, soooo useful! [NPC] Arthur: I have one in my Minion, look!|
|None|[NPC] Arthur: People always empty the storage of my Minion when I'm not looking!|

## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|

|None|[NPC] Aura: I'm going back to the drawing boards.|

# Baar
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Baar]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Gives the player [[The Alchemist's Staff]] and 250 Reputation ([[Factions]]) after giving various items.
Location: [[Dragontail Auction House]]
Coordinates: -638 123 -791
Baar is an [[NPC]] found above the [[Dragontail Auction House]]. They ask for various gold-themed items in exchange for [[The Alchemist's Staff]] and 250 [[Factions|Reputation]].
## Quest
The player needs to give Baar these resources in the following order:
  64x [[Gold Ingot]] 5x [[Golden Plate]] 1x [[Diver's Armor|Diver's Boots]] 1x [[Diver's Armor|Diver's Shirt]] 32x [[Enchanted Gold Block]] 5x [[Golden Powder]] 32x [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]] 1x [[Golden Ball]]
Which costs roughly from the [[Bazaar]], alongside a [[Snorkeling Armor|Snorkeling Shoes]] and [[Snorkeling Armor|Snorkeling Vest]]. After giving the [[Golden Ball]], the player will receive [[The Alchemist's Staff]] and 250 [[Factions|Reputation]] for their current [[Factions|Faction]].
This quest is shared with the [[Alchemage]], meaning that any progress contributed to one of the two [[NPC|NPCs]] remains on the other.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Not in a Faction ([[Factions]])|[NPC] Baar: Hello there, adventurer! [NPC] Baar: You look like someone who could help me with my research! [NPC] Baar: If you join our faction I might have some tasks for you. [NPC] Baar: I'll make it worth your while.|
|In the Mage Faction ([[Factions]]) without the quest already started|[NPC] Baar: You have no business talking to me.|
|Starting the quest|[NPC] Baar: Hello there, adventurer! [NPC] Baar: I'm the local alchemist, I do research on different types of gold. [NPC] Baar: If you have some time maybe you could help me with some tasks. [NPC] Baar: I'll make sure it's worth your time. [NPC] Baar: Talk to me again if you want to help out.|
|Needing 64 (1 stack) [[Gold Ingot]]|[NPC] Baar: To start out I'll need some generic gold to experiment on, could you get me a stack?|
|Needing 5 [[Golden Plate]]|[NPC] Baar: Next I need some flat gold to test how reflective gold is, could you try forging gold into 5 gold plates?|
|Needing 1 Diver's Boots ([[Diver's Armor]])|[NPC] Baar: I heard there exist golden boots that helps you swim faster in water. I would like to test their magical properties, could you bring me them?|
|Needing 1 Diver's Trunks ([[Diver's Armor]])|[NPC] Baar: It seems like the first piece you brought me is only 25% of the magical power, could you get me the leggings?|
|Needing 32 [[Enchanted Gold Block]]|[NPC] Baar: Now I need a lot of compacted gold, it has to be extremely dense. A half stack should do.|
|Needing 5 [[Golden Powder]]|[NPC] Baar: There is a fine-grained gold substance somewhere in the Hub, I'll need 5 of that.|
|Needing 32 [[Enchanted Golden Carrot]]|[NPC] Baar: Next I'm going to need a vegetable that is made out of solid gold. I want to experiment with how gold interacts with organics, maybe you could find some, like a half stack?|
|Needing 1 [[Golden Ball]]|[NPC] Baar: I just need one last thing, there's an extremely dangerous scientist who sells an assortment of items, he has a special rounded type of gold. Try to convince him to sell you it.|
|Giving 1 [[Golden Ball]]|[NPC] Baar: Finally, my research is complete! [NPC] Baar: You have single-handedly sped up my research by several years. [NPC] Baar: As promised, here is your reward.|
|After completing the quest|[NPC] Baar: If I ever need your help, I will be sure to let you know!|
|Wrong amount of an item given|[NPC] Baar: This isn't the correct amount of the item I asked for. [NPC] Baar: I need the EXACT amount I asked for!|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 1/4)|[NPC] Baar: Are you a Wizard? [NPC] Baar: Well you don't look like one, and you don't smell like one either. [NPC] Baar: In that case, I have nothing against you. I wish you a sulphuric day sir.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 2/4)|[NPC] Baar: Hello stranger. Are you looking to join our faction? [NPC] Baar: What a fantastic choice! [NPC] Baar: We have a range of motives far deeper and more interesting than those terrible fire mages. [NPC] Baar: Joining our faction is quite hard but I'm sure an incredible individual like you will manage!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 3/4)|[NPC] Baar: Sul-phur [NPC] Baar: Sulphur? [NPC] Baar: Me Sulphur.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 4/4)|[NPC] Baar: Are you a Hero? [NPC] Baar: Did you come to free us from the everlasting threat of the wizards? [NPC] Baar: The mighty Volcano is protecting us, but for how long?|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Baar: You seem suspicious.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Baar: Sorry, I can't talk to you right now.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Baar: Please stop bothering me.|

# Banker
Aliases: Belanor - Banker
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Banker]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Saving Up ([[Quests]])
Description: Allows the player to access the [[Bank]] to store or access their [[Coins]].
Location: [[Bank]]
Coordinates: -29.5 72 -38
Banker is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Bank]]. They grant the player access to the [[Bank]].
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Banker: Hello there! [NPC] Banker: You may want to store your Coins in a safe place while you are off adventuring. [NPC] Banker: Storing them in your Bank keeps them safe and allows you to earn interest at the start of every season!|
## Banking System
Right-clicking the Banker will open the [[Bank]] interface. The player will be presented with several options, consisting of the ability to deposit and withdraw [[Coins]] from either a personal account or an account shared with the player's co-op, viewing previous [[Bank|Banking]] activity, and upgrading the co-op [[Bank]] account.
[[Coins]] stored in the Bank are not lost when the player [[Death|dies]].
### Bank Upgrades
Players are able to upgrade their co-op bank account with [[Coins]] and [[Enchanted Gold Block|Enchanted Gold Blocks]] after meeting the respective [[Gold Ingot|Gold]] Collection and Museum Milestone requirement, which is viewable in the upgrade screen. Upgrading the [[Bank]] account increases the maximum amount of [[Coins]] able to be stored in the bank, and increases the maximum interest able to be gained. Upgrading Bank accounts also rewards [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
At least 10,000,000 [[Coins]] must be stored in the co-op bank and the purse combined before the upgrade screen is unlocked.
Upgrading your co-op account will also upgrade your personal account. Your personal account's max amount will always be 20% of your co-op account's max.
## Trivia
- Unlike most other NPCs, Banker stands on the verge of 2 blocks, rather than in the middle of one.
- Other islands feature other Banker NPCs, serving the same purpose, namely [[Banker Barry]] or [[Banker Broadjaw]], [[Belanor]] or [[Bruto]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Banker.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Moved Banker from -24.5, 71, -58.5 ([[Coordinates]]) to -29.5, 72, -38 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

# Banker Barry
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Events, Season of Jerry
[Banker Barry]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to access the [[Bank]].
Location: [[Jerry's Workshop]]
Coordinates: 20.5 77 44.5
Banker Barry is an [[NPC]] located in [[Jerry's Workshop]]. They manage the [[Bank|Banking System]], allowing the player to deposit and withdraw [[Coins]], along with looking at previous transactions.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Banking System
Right-clicking Banker Barry will open the [[Bank]] interface. The player will be presented with several options, consisting of the ability to deposit and withdraw [[Coins]], viewing previous [[Bank|Banking]] activity, and upgrading the [[Bank]] account.
[[Coins]] stored in the Bank are not lost when the player [[Death|dies]].
### Bank Upgrades
Players are able to upgrade their bank account with [[Coins]] and [[Enchanted Gold Block|Enchanted Gold Blocks]] after meeting the respective requirement in the [[Gold Ingot|Gold]] Collection. Upgrading the [[Bank]] account increases the maximum amount of [[Coins]] able to be stored in the bank, and increases the maximum interest able to be gained. Upgrading Bank accounts also rewards [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
At least 10,000,000 [[Coins]] must be stored in the bank and the purse combined before the upgrade screen is unlocked.
## Trivia
- Interacting with Banker Barry will reset the player's [[Personal Bank]] cooldown, if the player has one.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Added Banker Barry.|

# Barry

Barry is an [[NPC]] found in [[Barry HQ]], and was formerly a [[Mayor Election|Mayor]], followed by an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wizard Tower]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Barry: All these protestors upstairs hate me! [NPC] Barry: And I don't know why! Select an option: [You could just ask them why.]|
|Selecting [You could just ask them why.]|[NPC] Barry: Of course [player], but I can't! [NPC] Barry: I'm stuck here planning my campaign for the next Rift election. [NPC] Barry: I don't have time to hear out the populace... Select an option: [Hearing them out would help with the election.]|
|Selecting [Hearing them out would help with the election.]|[NPC] Barry: Haha, that's crazy... [NPC] Barry: So crazy it might just work! [NPC] Barry: Mmhh... [NPC] Barry: Okay, let's do it. [NPC] Barry: Go and hear a protestor's complaint. [NPC] Barry: Then, come back to me so we can resolve their issue.|
|Before hearing a protestor's complaint|[NPC] Barry: Go and hear a protestor's complaint. [NPC] Barry: Then, come back to me so we can resolve their issue.|
|[[Creed]]'s complaint|[NPC] Barry: Oh Creed is still asking for Motes? [NPC] Barry: 300 Motes?! [NPC] Barry: My government cannot afford this!|
|[[Frankie]]'s complaint|[NPC] Barry: Frankie is lagging? [NPC] Barry: I suppose that's technically my fault, with all those new people in the Rift lately. [NPC] Barry: Maybe they are using a VPN, or downloading something, or just on a bad proxy. Select an option: [What...?]|
|Selecting [What...?]|[NPC] Barry: Just ask Frankie, he'll understand.|
|[[Soma]]'s complaint|[NPC] Barry: Soma is concerned about the 50% tax rates? [NPC] Barry: Well, she has to understand that Tax Brackets are part of a progressive tax system. Select an option: [Huhhh]|
|Selecting [Huhhh]|[NPC] Barry: There are currently 5 different federal tax brackets in the Rift. [NPC] Barry: Each with rates respectively, 10%, 20%, 30%, 40% and 50%. Select an option: [Slow down please.]|
|Selecting [Slow down please.]|[NPC] Barry: The first bracket is for anyone earning below 20,000 Motes per revolution. [NPC] Barry: The 2nd one is between 20,000 and 40,000. [NPC] Barry: So for example if you earn 40,000... [NPC] Barry: You will pay 10% on your first 20,000 Motes, so 2,000. [NPC] Barry: And you will be taxed 20% on the next 20,000, so 4,000 extra. [NPC] Barry: In total you will have to pay 6,000 Motes. Select an option: [Maybe you should explain that to Soma yourself...]|
|Selecting [Maybe you should explain that to Soma yourself...]|[NPC] Barry: The 3rd bracket is between 40,000 and 70,000. [NPC] Barry: The 4th bracket is between 70,000 and 100,000. [NPC] Barry: The 5th and final bracket is anything above 100,000, at 50% tax rate. Select an option: [Why don't you just tell Soma what she owes?]|
|Selecting [Why don't you just tell Soma what she owes?]|[NPC] Barry: Sorry, we only tell her if she didn't pay enough! [NPC] Barry: Now that you are armed with accounting knowledge, go help Soma!|
|[[Harriette]]'s complaint|[NPC] Barry: Harriette said that I want to ban jumping? [NPC] Barry: Wait... [NPC] Barry: Harriette said that the Rift Herald said that I want to ban jumping... [NPC] Barry: It's just not true, I never said anything like that! [NPC] Barry: She knows the Rift Herald is a newspaper owned by the family, right? [NPC] Barry: They'll bribe, lie and DEFAME anybody just to get votes!|
|[[Violetta]]'s complaint|[NPC] Barry: Violetta needs help with her math homework again?! [NPC] Barry: Those questions keep getting tougher and tougher... [NPC] Barry: Can you help her out yourself? [NPC] Barry: I do magic, not science!|
|[[Stain]]'s complaint|[NPC] Barry: Stain said what? [NPC] Barry: But I'm not Berry... [NPC] Barry: I'm BARRY! With an A!|
|[[Dust]]'s complaint|[NPC] Barry: Dust wants me to fix his mistakes? [NPC] Barry: It sounds like it's too late, I don't know what to tell him. [NPC] Barry: Just... tell him something smart! [NPC] Barry: It might just do the trick.|
|After helping all the protestors|[NPC] Barry: Thanks to your help, all of the protestors have decided to help the community instead of just complaining! [NPC] Barry: Here, I want you to take my favorite pen. [NPC] Barry: I don't need it anymore since this whole universe is on computers anyway.|
|Without owning [[Barry's Montgray Pen]] or [[SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm]]|[NPC] Barry: Hey, need a pen?|
|While owning [[Barry's Montgray Pen]] or [[SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm]]|[NPC] Barry: All of us here are preparing for the next Rift election. [NPC] Barry: With some luck, our government may stay in place for an eternity!|
### Removed

|-|-|
|In [[Wizard Tower]]|[NPC] Barry: Running for Mayor office, I have many times. [NPC] Barry: City Projects for coins towards wizards, I tried organizing. [NPC] Barry: Embezzled it all away, Scorpius did. [NPC] Barry: They will see when I turn the world upside down!|
|During Repair Wizard Portal ([[City Projects]])|[NPC] Barry: Portal complete, it nearly is. [NPC] Barry: Mayor become, again I will. [NPC] Barry: Embezzling, not this time.|

# Bednom
Categories: Non-player characters, Dwarven Mines
[Bednom]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Talks to the player about their strange dreams, which ultimately lead to a loot chest by the [[Cliffside Veins]].
Location: [[Dwarven Village]]
Coordinates: -30 213.843 -89
Bednom is an [[NPC]] in the [[Dwarven Village]] who tells his strange dreams to the player.
## Mechanics
Bednom gains new dialogue upon talking to him on 3 separate [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Days]].
On the third and final day of talking to the NPC, Bednom will say that there is a treasure buried in loose rocks next to a bridge. The player must then go to [[Coordinates|-12, 127, 57]] and mine the gravel block there. The bridge that Bednom talks about is the Divan's Gateway, which connects the Cliffside Veins with the Great Ice Wall, and the treasure location is the gravel near [[Bylma]], the NPC by the bridge. A chest will appear, containing the following loot:
- 500 (7 stacks plus 52) [[Mithril Powder]]
- 3 [[Enchanted Titanium]]
- 7 [[Enchanted Gold Ingot]]
- 3 [[Enchanted Diamond]]
- 10 [[Enchanted Mithril]]
Completing Bednom's quest will award the player with the [[Achievements|"Nightmare" Achievement]].
## Nightmare Nullifier
The [[Nightmare Nullifier]] is obtained by giving Bednom 1 [[Black Coffee]] and waiting 1 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Day]]. Bednom's quest must be completed first.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Day 1|[NPC] Bednom: I just had a very very strange dream... [NPC] Bednom: In my dream I flew out of my body, went through some kind of screen barrier. [NPC] Bednom: I turned and I could see myself standing behind that screen, right here. [NPC] Bednom: Around me were strange objects everywhere, but the strangest part was the giant. [NPC] Bednom: A Giant! Sitting in front of that screen, watching me. [NPC] Bednom: The giant seemed to speak a foreign language, they screamed "POGGERS!" [NPC] Bednom: Then I woke up in a sweat. [NPC] Bednom: I'm really upside down, I need time to think. [NPC] Bednom: Thanks for listening.|
|Day 2|[NPC] Bednom: I had another nightmare, very similar to the previous one. [NPC] Bednom: I could see myself behind the screen again, it looked like I was talking. [NPC] Bednom: I couldn't hear it, but the words appeared on the screen. [NPC] Bednom: The exact words I told you last time. [NPC] Bednom: So weird, so weird.... I need time to think.|
|Day 3|[NPC] Bednom: Once again, I had the same recurring nightmare, a little different this time. [NPC] Bednom: I was talking behind the screen again, I mentioned a treasure. [NPC] Bednom: I knew the location of a treasure right right close to a giant Dwarf Statue. [NPC] Bednom: Buried in loose rocks next to a bridge I said. [NPC] Bednom: But it makes no sense, I've never even heard of such a treasure!!|
|Before Finding Treasure|[NPC] Bednom: Dwarf Statue, bridge,, loose rocks, treasure...? What a strange dream...|
|After Finding Treasure|[NPC] Bednom: The dreams stopped, it'll forever remain a mystery.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Bednom: Last night I had another horrible nightmare. I'm afraid to fall asleep again.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Bednom: Thank you. I'll make a strong brew from this that'll keep me awake all night.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Bednom: Tough love, but maybe you're right. They're only nightmares, right? Not real. Heh.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None; Still on Day 1/Day 2|[NPC] Bednom: I wonder if I'll have that nightmare again tonight...|
|None; Still on Day 1/Day 2|[NPC] Bednom: Wow that dream was weird.|
|None; Still on Day 1/Day 2|[NPC] Bednom: I'm still thinking about that dream I had.|
|Condition|Dialogue|
|Giving Bednom Cheap, Decent, or Black Coffee|[NPC] Bednom: What's this? A brew? [NPC] Bednom: I need to drink this? Select an option: [Yes]|
|Selecting [Yes]; Cheap or Decent Coffee|[NPC] Bednom: Okay well, I trust you. [NPC] Bednom: gulp [NPC] Bednom: Meh, I doubt this will do anything...|
|Selecting [Yes]; Black Coffee|[NPC] Bednom: Okay well, I trust you. [NPC] Bednom: gulp [NPC] Bednom: Holy crap this tastes terrible!!! [NPC] Bednom: What's the point!!!! Select an option: [You'll see]|
|Selecting [You'll see]|[NPC] Bednom: I guess it was rhetorical, uh?|
|Waiting One SkyBlock Day|[NPC] Bednom: WOW!!!! [NPC] Bednom: I REALLY CAN'T SLEEP AT ALL!!!! [NPC] Bednom: THAT'S FANTASTIC!!!! [NPC] Bednom: NO MORE NIGHTMARES!!!! [NPC] Bednom: HERE TAKE THIS!!!!|
|Talking to him later than the next; SkyBlock Day (and not getting the reward)|[NPC] Bednom: That drink you gave me the other day... [NPC] Bednom: It was amazing! I couldn't have nightmares that night! [NPC] Bednom: You should give me another and visit in the morning! [NPC] Bednom: The dreams stopped, it'll forever remain a mystery.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|After Claiming [[Nightmare Nullifier]]|[NPC] Bednom: AM I TOO LOUD??!! [NPC] Bednom: I HAD NO IDEA I COULD JUST STAY AWAKE, IT'S GENIUS!!! [NPC] Bednom: WHY SLEEP WHEN YOU CAN JUST STAY AWAKE FOREVER!!!!|
## Tips
- Bednom will only gain new dialogue after waiting a full SkyBlock Day. It is not enough to simply talk to him again at midnight the next day.
## Trivia
- Bednom is one of four NPCs mentioned during Cult of the Fallen Star Meeting #5, where Thondin states, "Bednom always has a nightmare" as one of several examples suggesting that every NPC lives in a simulation.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Dec 5, 2025|Added Bednom as a Garden Visitor ([[The Garden]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Bednom.|
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added Bednom as a Garden Visitor ([[The Garden]]).|

## Dialogue
