## Banking System
Right-clicking Belanor will open the [[Bank]] interface. The player will be presented with several options, consisting of the ability to deposit and withdraw [[Coins]], viewing previous [[Bank|Banking]] activity, and upgrading the [[Bank]] account.
[[Coins]] stored in the Bank are not lost when the player [[Death|dies]].
### Bank Upgrades
Players are able to upgrade their bank account with [[Coins]] and [[Enchanted Gold Block|Enchanted Gold Blocks]] after meeting the respective requirement in the [[Gold Ingot|Gold]] Collection. Upgrading the [[Bank]] account increases the maximum amount of [[Coins]] able to be stored in the bank, and increases the maximum interest able to be gained. Upgrading Bank accounts also rewards [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
At least 10,000,000 [[Coins]] must be stored in the bank and the purse combined before the upgrade screen is unlocked.

|Mar 8, 2022|Added Belanor.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Beth
Categories: Non-player characters
[Beth]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Shady Jake ([[Quests]]); Further Investigating Jake ([[Quests]])
[Locations]
Description: Has suspicious about [[Jake]].
Location: [[Desert Settlement]]
Coordinates: 163.5 77 -359
Description: Appears here after the player has accepted 4 of their visiting offers on [[The Garden]].
Location: [[Underground Lab]]
Coordinates: 174.5 26 -477
Description: Appears here after the player has accepted 5 of their visiting offers on [[The Garden]].
Location: [[Underground Lab]]
Coordinates: 173.875 28 -498.5
Description: Appears here after the player presses the lever in the first room of the lab, and before moving around to explore this second room.
Location: [[Underground Lab]]
Coordinates: 190.5 22 -499.5
Beth is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Desert Settlement]]. She has a quest involving [[Jake]], where she and the player explore the [[Underground Lab]] and uncover [[Jake]]'s secrets. The first visit there unlocks [[Jake's Plushie]], and the second visit, which requires unlocking the [[Greenhouse]] first, grants access to the [[Crop Analyzer]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[Desert Settlement]] None|[NPC] Beth: That Jake fellow is a bit suspicious. [NPC] Beth: He says he can't leave his house but I've seen him walking around all the time...|
|[[Desert Settlement]] During the quest, before receiving the coordinates|[NPC] Beth: I keep thinking about Jake's behavior... [NPC] Beth: I know I'll catch him doing something!|
|[[Desert Settlement]] During the quest's coordinate step, before breaking the block|[NPC] Beth: Did you figure out the meaning of the note yet? [NPC] Beth: Maybe you should go to the cave where I followed him. I remember there were a lot of mushrooms.|
|[[Desert Settlement]] After first discovering the lab|[NPC] Beth: Seems that I have misjudged Jake, he might not have been as suspicious as I thought. [NPC] Beth: The plushie he gave me is quite cute actually!|
|[[Desert Settlement]] After completing the quest|[NPC] Beth: Thanks again for helping me discovering the truth about Jake. [NPC] Beth: Can't believe he had a second secret lab to hide!|
|[[The Garden]] First Visit|[NPC] Beth: I think I am really onto something here. [NPC] Beth: Jake says he can't walk, but I've seen him walk yesterday. [NPC] Beth: He also asks strangers to get him some animals, but these animals are nowhere to be found!|
|[[The Garden]] Accepting First Visit|[NPC] Beth: I'm getting really close to solving this mystery.|
|[[The Garden]] Second Visit|[NPC] Beth: I followed him today to where he brings all the animals he catches. He goes down some weird cave...|
|[[The Garden]] Accepting Second Visit|[NPC] Beth: Unfortunately, I couldn't follow him any further as he almost noticed me, so I backed off for now.|
|[[The Garden]] Third Visit|[NPC] Beth: I managed to follow him into the cave today, he had a shovel with him and a note fell out of his pocket...|
|[[The Garden]] Accepting Third Visit|[NPC] Beth: The note reads: "175 42 -470" [NPC] Beth: Not really sure what these numbers mean yet, do you have any ideas?|
|[[The Garden]] Accepting Third Visit|Beth will not visit the garden again until the player digs down at the coordinates.|
|[[The Garden]] Fourth Visit|[NPC] Beth: You found what? A secret lab hidden inside the cave???|
|[[The Garden]] Accepting Fourth Visit|[NPC] Beth: We need to check this out together. I'll meet you there!|
|[[The Garden]] Accepting Fourth Visit|Beth will not visit the garden again until the player goes back to the lab.|
|[[The Garden]] Fifth Visit|[NPC] Beth: Hey, <player>, I've been thinking a lot about that lab we found... I think there's more to it than just the plushies.|
|[[The Garden]] Accepting Fifth Visit|[NPC] Beth: Especially with all those strange machines and experiments. I need your help again... let's meet back in the lab.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Beth: Maybe I was wrong to trust you...|
|[[The Garden]] If a [[Co-op]] member has the quest|[NPC] Beth: Hi! I have a quest for a member of your co-op! Tell them to come see me!|
|[[Underground Lab]] First Visit|[NPC] Beth: This is so strange... [NPC] Beth: I can't believe this has been down here for all this time... I am speechless. [NPC] Beth: WHAT? There are animals inside of here?!?! Jake is doing horrible experiments with these animals that he catches... [NPC] Beth: He has gone completely mad! He's a psychopath! [NPC] Beth: And look at all of these tools he has laying around here... a SAW?... a HAMMER? [NPC] Beth: What is this psycho doing with these animals??? [NPC] Beth: Jake has a lot of explaining to do! [NPC] Jake: ... BETH?? AND &&<capitalized player name>, WHAT ARE YOU GUYS DOING IN HERE??? [NPC] Jake: IT'S NOT WHAT IT LOOKS LIKE! [NPC] Beth: WE KNOW WHAT YOU ARE DOING IN HERE, YOU PSYCHO! YOU WILL PAY FOR WHAT YOU ARE DOING TO THESE ANIMALS! [NPC] Jake: ANIMALS? I CAN EXPLAIN, I SWEAR! [NPC] Beth: WELL GO ON THEN, EXPLAIN YOURSELF! [NPC] Jake: I don't know what you guys think I am doing here, but this is just my workshop! [NPC] Jake: I am just making toys for animals... [NPC] Beth: Oh really? Do you really think we are that naive? [NPC] Beth: We know that you are experimenting with animals down here, how do you explain this tank? [NPC] Jake: Those are my plushies! I preserve them in Jelly so they stay fresh! [NPC] Jake: They look super realistic, but no animal was harmed when making them! [NPC] Jake: I can prove it! Here, take this. This is one of the plushies I made, you can have it! [NPC] Jake: So really, I am not harming them at all! [NPC] Beth: Everything makes sense now...I'm sorry I yelled at you Jake. I'm so sorry. [NPC] Jake: It's alright now. I was wrong to lie about it and I should have told you sooner. [NPC] Beth: <player>, thank you so much for telling me what you saw here. I'll be forever grateful. [NPC] Beth: I have to go now, I hope to see you guys soon. [NPC] Jake: That was a close one, thank you <player> for having my back. Anyway, I need to go back to my business.|
|[[Underground Lab]] Start of the second visit|[NPC] Beth: This place still gives me chills... but I'm sure there's more to uncover. [NPC] Beth: We know Jake's been hiding things, and I think we're getting closer to the truth. [NPC] Beth: Hmm, this corner... I don't think I've noticed this before. [NPC] Beth: There's something strange about this wall. It looks out of place... [NPC] Beth: Wait, there's a lever here! [NPC] Beth: What is this? A hidden passage? I knew Jake was hiding something from me, but this... this is something different. [NPC] Beth: I don't know if we should trust this, but we've come this far... Let's see what's behind it.|
|[[Underground Lab]] Before pressing the lever|[NPC] Beth: See if you can pull down this lever, it's a bit stuck.|
|[[Underground Lab]] After pressing the lever|[NPC] Beth: I... I had no idea this was here. What could Jake be hiding in this secret part of the lab? [NPC] Beth: It's very dark in here, where's the light-switch in this place? [NPC] Beth: Oh... my... what is all of this? [NPC] Beth: This is... this isn't just about plushies anymore. Jake's been working on something much bigger. [NPC] Beth: Hybridization? Mutation? What is this madness? He's experimenting on something... [NPC] Beth: Resilient livestock... enhanced growth rates... Jake, what have you done? [NPC] Beth: These aren't normal crops. These are mutated... just like the ones we've been finding in the Garden. But why hide all of this down here? A door opens from within the lab, and Jake shows up [NPC] Jake: Beth... I told you to stay out of this. [NPC] Beth: Jake! You have a lot of explaining to do, once again... What is all of this? What are you doing down here? [NPC] Beth: Please tell me the truth this time Jake, I can't take all these lies anymore! [NPC] Jake: It's... it's not what it looks like. This lab... it's for research. To improve farming techniques, to help both crops and animals adapt to new environments. [NPC] Beth: Research? This doesn't look like research, Jake! This looks like you are playing with life itself. Those tanks, those animals, what are you actually doing to them? [NPC] Jake: You don't understand, Beth! Farming as we know is evolving. If we don't adapt, we'll be left behind. The world's demands are growing, and so are the challenges. [NPC] Beth: That doesn't give you the right to hide this from everyone. What about the risks? The ethics? [NPC] Beth: You're creating something entirely unnatural. [NPC] Jake: sigh I know it seems... wrong. But these experiments could change everything. [NPC] Jake: Imagine crops that can survive the harshest conditions, or animals that never get sick. [NPC] Jake: It's not just about survival, it's about thriving. [NPC] Beth: And what about the things you are not telling me? What else are you hiding down here, Jake? [NPC] Jake: There are some things you're better off not knowing. Not yet at least. [NPC] Beth: No, Jake. I've come this far, and I won't stop now. If you won't tell me the truth, I'll find it myself. [NPC] Jake: Beth, you don't understand, some things aren't ready to be uncovered. It's not about keeping secrets, it's about protecting what I've been working towards. [NPC] Beth: Protecting what? Your pride? Your experiments? This isn't just about you, Jake. We have a responsibility to use this lab for good, not to bury it under layers of secrecy. [NPC] Beth: What's this? [NPC] Jake: That... is the Crop Analyzer. [NPC] Beth: The Crop Analyzer? What does it do? [NPC] Jake: It's a tool I've been developing. It analyzes mutations in crops, helps identify traits that can improve yield, resistance or even flavor. [NPC] Jake: But it's not finished. It's delicate work, Beth. Work that requires precision, and data. [NPC] Beth: Work that shouldn't be done in isolation. Jake, this could change everything for farmers everywhere. Why are you hiding something so revolutionary? [NPC] Jake: Because it's not perfect yet. A mistake could have disastrous consequences. I didn't want to involve anyone until I was sure it was ready. [NPC] Beth: Jake, you've done incredible work here. But you don't have to do it alone anymore. Let me and <player> help you. [NPC] Beth: Together, we can finish this and make sure it's used responsibly. [NPC] Jake: Alright. You're right, Beth. I've been trying to carry this burden alone for too long. If we're going to make this work, we'll do it together. [NPC] Jake: And you, <player>, you've been instrumental in getting us to this point. You should have access to the Crop Analyzer. You've earned my trust.|
## Trivia
- Beth is one of the [[NPC|NPCs]] the player must talk to during the quest [[Quests|Talk to the Villager Inhabitants]].
- After having Beth visit the player's [[The Garden|Garden]] 3 times, the player can dig a block at [[Coordinates|175, 42, -470]] in the [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]] and find a hidden base. On her 4th visit, the player can go back to the base and claim [[Jake's Plushie]].

Bramass Beastslayer is an [[NPC]] located on [[Spider's Den]], outside of [[Grandma's House]], on the top of the hill. They introduce the player to the [[Bestiary]] and grant [[Beastslayer's Codex]] after reaching [[Bestiary]] Milestone 50..

|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: Hello, adventurer! I am Bramass Beastslayer! I've slain beasts of all sorts across SkyBlock! [NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: I sure wish there was a record of all my accomplishments in one place! [NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: Oh wait...there is! [NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: Your Bestiary is a compendium of all of the mobs in SkyBlock! [NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: View your mob stats, unlock rewards, and more! [NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: You can find the Bestiary in your Combat Skill menu!|
|After reaching [[Bestiary]] Milestone 50|[NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: Oh, you reached Bestiary Milestone 50! Few adventurers make it so far, especially in one piece... [NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: You're certainly worthy. Here, I want you to have this.|
|After claiming [[Beastslayer's Codex]]|[NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: That Beastslayer's Codex increases its Stats and Rarity as you reach more Bestiary Milestones. [NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: It has served me throughout my travels, but those days are now long behind me. [NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: Now, go and search the lands far and wide for more mobs to slay - that is the true calling of a Beastmaster!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: Killing enough mobs in a given Family unlocks rewards. [NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: You can unlock ✯ Magic Find and ❁ Strength bonuses, and loot drop information for that Family!|
|None|[NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: Reach Milestones in your Bestiary by unlocking unique Family tiers. [NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: Reaching Milestones rewards ❤ Health, Combat Exp, and more!|
|None|[NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: You can always view your Bestiary in your Combat Skill menu!|
|None|[NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: Killing mobs in a Family enough times rewards you with ✯ Magic Find towards that mob! [NPC] Bramass Beastslayer: This increases your chance to find rare loot when killing this mob!|

|After completing Trial of Blue Flames II|[NPC] Brian: Wow! That campfire seems to scare off most foragers, but not you! [NPC] Brian: Here's your Soul Campfire Initiate Badge I! [NPC] Brian: Complete the Trial of Blue Flames III, and I'll upgrade your badge!|

|(same as above)|[NPC] Brian: Congrats! Your (Soul Campfire Badge) was upgraded to a (Soul Campfire Badge)! [NPC] Brian: Try the Trial of Blue Flames # for a greater challenge! [NPC] Brian: The rarity of your item will be upgraded on the next trial!|
|After completing a Trial of Blue Flames which resulted in a rarity upgrade|[NPC] Brian: Congrats on completing tier #! [NPC] Brian: You are ready for the next badge rarity! [NPC] Brian: To receive it, we require (Materials)!|

|(same as above)|[NPC] Brian: Congrats! Your (Soul Campfire Badge) was upgraded to a (Soul Campfire Badge)! [NPC] Brian: Try the Trial of Blue Flames Trial # for a greater challenge!|
|After completing all Trials of Blue Flames|[NPC] Brian: Congratulations on beating every single Trial of Blue Flames! You're the real fire guy, hotshot!|

# Brigette
Aliases: Bridgette
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Brigette]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Safety First ([[Quests]])
[Locations]
Description: Starts a quest regarding account security.
Location: [[Security Hall]]
Coordinates: -17.5 46 -118.6
Description: Teaches the player about account security.
Location: [[Security Hall]]
Coordinates: -18.5 45.5 -92.5
Brigette is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Security Hall]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Quest Unavailable|[NPC] Brigette: I'm a little busy right now... come back soon!|
|First interaction|[NPC] Brigette: Hey, recruit! [NPC] Brigette: Come this way.|
|First interaction|Brigette will then walk to a chair in the Briefing Room.|
|After sitting in the chair|[NPC] Brigette: Here, take a seat. [NPC] Brigette: Welcome to the Security Hall. It's the place for all safety matters. [NPC] Brigette: I believe Elizabeth has already introduced you to what we do. [NPC] Brigette: The goal of the Safety Squad is to keep SkyBlock safe, and it starts with you. Select an option: [Who, me?]|
|Selecting [Who, me?]|[NPC] Brigette: Yes, you! [NPC] Brigette: If everyone is aware of the importance of safety, the number of safety-related incidents would greatly decrease. Select an option: [What is a safety-related incident anyway?]|
|Selecting [What is a safety-related incident anyway?]|[NPC] Brigette: I'm glad you asked. [NPC] Brigette: On one hand, it is very important to do things to keep yourself as safe as possible. [NPC] Brigette: Stay indoors when there's a thunderstorm, wear a safety hat while at a construction site, washing your hands frequently while in a hospital... [NPC] Brigette: These are all examples of physical safety. [NPC] Brigette: Today, however, we will be focused on something that's also very important. [NPC] Brigette: That being - Account Safety. Select an option: [What's the point of Account Safety?]|
|Selecting [What's the point of Account Safety?]|[NPC] Brigette: Imagine logging in to SkyBlock and finding that all your belongings have disappeared. [NPC] Brigette: Or even worse, trying to log into your Minecraft account but finding yourself locked out.|
|Selecting [What's the point of Account Safety?]|The player will be teleported to the nearby jail cell, seeing a mannequin of their own skin punching the iron bars from the other side. Several dropped items float around their feet.|
|After teleporting the player to the nearby jail cell|[NPC] Brigette: The point of Account Safety is to prevent this from happening. Select an option: [I don't want to lose my account!]|
|Selecting [I don't want to lose my account!]|[NPC] Brigette: And that's why you should pay close attention to today's briefing. [NPC] Brigette: SkyBlock has a lot of players. [NPC] Brigette: You will meet a lot of them throughout playing SkyBlock. [NPC] Brigette: You could even make some very good friends! [NPC] Brigette: However, not everyone is here to make friends. [NPC] Brigette: Some of them are here to steal your coins, items, or even your account! Select an option: [That doesn't sound good.]|
|Selecting [That doesn't sound good.]|[NPC] Brigette: Of course it doesn't. [NPC] Brigette: Rule number 1, never trust strangers on the internet. [NPC] Brigette: Strangers are people you just met. You don't know who they are. Select an option: [Stranger danger, understood.]|
|Selecting [Stranger danger, understood.]|[NPC] Brigette: If someone approaches you, they very likely want something from you. [NPC] Brigette: What looks like a seemingly innocent request could lead to you losing your account. [NPC] Brigette: The most common way to lose your account is by opening suspicious links. [NPC] Brigette: If someone sends a link in chat, chances are they are trying to steal your account. Select an option: [I'm not stupid enough to open a random link!]|
|Selecting [I'm not stupid enough to open a random link!]|[NPC] Brigette: I'm sure you're not! [NPC] Brigette: The thing is, the evil people who steal accounts will try to catch you off guard. [NPC] Brigette: They try to make you open links in situations where it seems reasonable. [NPC] Brigette: Or situations where you're distracted, or least suspecting a scam. Select an option: [C'mon, I'm sure it's not thaaat complicated.]|
|Selecting [C'mon, I'm sure it's not thaaat complicated.]|[NPC] Brigette: A way hackers can gain your trust is by messaging you from an account they've already hacked. [NPC] Brigette: If it looks like your friend sent you that message, you'd be inclined to trust them more, right? [NPC] Brigette: Then when you click the link they send you...|
|Selecting [C'mon, I'm sure it's not thaaat complicated.]|A ping is heard, and the following dialogue appears with noticeably less delay than other lines of dialogue.|
|After the ping sound plays|[NPC] Brigette: Just like that, your account is gone. Select an option: [Brigette, you're quite scary.]|
|Selecting [Brigette, you're quite scary.]|[NPC] Brigette: Scary I am indeed. [NPC] Brigette: But losing your account is an even scarier proposition. [NPC] Brigette: Anyways. [NPC] Brigette: We were talking about links, and how you should be very careful when clicking on links. [NPC] Brigette: If you ever receive a suspicious message in-game, you should report the player with the /report command. Select an option: [The /report command. Noted.]|
|Selecting [The /report command. Noted.]|[NPC] Brigette: The dangers of clicking unverified links is not limited to within SkyBlock. [NPC] Brigette: It's something that applies all over the internet, really. [NPC] Brigette: There are some situations where you may encounter suspicious links for SkyBlock outside of the game itself. Select an option: [Links for SkyBlock outside SkyBlock? Like what?]|
|Selecting [Links for SkyBlock outside SkyBlock? Like what?]|[NPC] Brigette: For example, you may be trying to download a mod for SkyBlock. [NPC] Brigette: Or perhaps, you're linked a page to someone's SkyBlock profile, to check out their stats or something. [NPC] Brigette: While it's unlikely that these links are bad, you should be careful when clicking them, and try your best to verify whether they're trustworthy. Select an option: [I will be careful when clicking on links, I promise!]|
|Selecting [I will be careful when clicking on links, I promise!]|[NPC] Brigette: That's the spirit! RING... RING... RING... RING... RING... RING... [NPC] Brigette: You're saying we got another recruit? That's great, I'll get them settled. [NPC] Brigette: Thanks, Salad! [NPC] Charlotte: It's Charlotte, not Salad!!|
|(same as above)|[[Jimmy]] will then teleport next to the player. As the camera is moved to look at them, the other chair breaks underneath them and sticks float around Jimmy's feet.|
|After Jimmy breaks the adjacent chair|[NPC] Brigette: Well that's not good! [NPC] Brigette: I hope you both are okay. Select an option: [I'm good, thanks.]|
|Selecting I'm good, thanks.|[NPC] Brigette: Sorry <username>, I'll go grab a chair real quick, and get someone to clean up this mess. [NPC] Brigette: New recruit, you may use my chair for now. [NPC] Brigette: Be right back! NEW OBJECTIVE Talk to Jimmy|
|Selecting I'm good, thanks.|The player will then be instructed to talk to [[Jimmy]], before Brigette returns to detain him.|
|After Selecting [Wait, what?] during [[Jimmy]]'s dialogue|[NPC] Brigette: STOP RIGHT THERE! [NPC] Brigette: That's not a recruit, he's supposed to be a detainee! [NPC] Jimmy: Aw shucks, I was so close! [NPC] Jimmy: I'll try again next time. [NPC] Jimmy: You haven't seen the last of me! [NPC] Brigette: I'm so sorry about putting you in danger like that. [NPC] Brigette: Are you alright? Select an option: [What just happened?]|
|Selecting [What just happened?]|[NPC] Brigette: Jimmy is currently wanted for twelve counts of scamming and five more counts of assault. [NPC] Brigette: Who knows what he could have done to you. [NPC] Brigette: ... [NPC] Brigette: I heard you were going to trade with Jimmy? Select an option: [Yeah].|
|Selecting [Yeah.]|[NPC] Brigette: Lucky for you, he couldn't pull off his scam within the Security Hall. [NPC] Brigette: Jimmy wanted you to trade outside so that he wouldn't get caught. Select an option: [How does this scam work?]|
|Selecting [How does this scam work?]|[NPC] Brigette: Jimmy would show you his "very cool gauntlets" in the trading menu. [NPC] Brigette: When you attempt to accept the trade, he would quickly swap it to something much cheaper. [NPC] Brigette: If the trade goes through, you'd be stuck with the cheaper item! Select an option: [Yikes.]|
|Selecting [Yikes.]|[NPC] Brigette: There are many other forms of these scams. [NPC] Brigette: Some people will set frequently used items up for auctions at a high price, hoping that someone accidentally clicks on them. [NPC] Brigette: Some people will pretend they can help you reforge something, then just steal your item once you handed it over to them. [NPC] Brigette: There's even scarier things, like someone telling you to click a link to join a giveaway. [NPC] Brigette: It will ask you to enter your Microsoft email and sends you a login code. [NPC] Brigette: If you give them your code, you've just lost your account entirely. [NPC] Brigette: There are a lot of scams lurking around every corner. [NPC] Brigette: We try our best to prevent scams from happening, but scammers always come up with new tricks. [NPC] Brigette: That's why it's always important to stay alert, and stay safe. [NPC] Brigette: Be especially wary of people you just met! [NPC] Brigette: Anyhow, that's all for today.|
|During the Quiz|[NPC] Brigette: I hope you've learnt quite a bit about account security. [NPC] Brigette: To cap it off, we have a short quiz to recap what we just went through. [NPC] Brigette: Answer a few questions, and you'll become a certified Safety Advocate! Question #1 Why is account security important? @ It can lead you to lose a lot of items and money. @ It can lead you to lose access to your Minecraft account. @ It can lead you to lose your Hypixel SkyBlock profile.|
|Selecting any answer|[NPC] Brigette: Correct! In fact, all of the listed statements are true - losing your account can mean lost items, money, profiles, or even your whole Microsoft account. Question #2 If someone in your party asks you to join their Discord to voice chat, and sends the invite code in chat without the "discord.gg", what should you do? @ Manually type the invite link in the browser and join the discord. @ Leave the party and report the player. @ Add the player on discord and ask for the invite link there.|
|Selecting Manually type the invite link in the browser and join the discord.|Brigette: DON'T DO THAT! You should not accept discord invites from players - they can be used to steal your account information.|
|(same as above)|The same question prompt will repeat.|
|Selecting Add the player on discord and ask for the invite link there.|Brigette: DON'T DO THAT! Adding people you don't know on Discord is generally not a good idea.|
|(same as above)|The same question prompt will repeat.|
|Selecting Leave the party and report the player.|[NPC] Brigette: YOU GOT IT! You can report the player with the /report command, followed by the player's username. Question #3 Your friend sends you a link telling you to go vote for their prize, You click the link, and it's asking you to log in with an account. What should you do? @ Register for an account and log in. @ Use a linked account (Google, Facebook etc.) to log in. @ Look for signs of your friend getting hacked, and don't click on that website any further.|
|Selecting Register for an account and log in.|Brigette: DON'T DO THAT! By registering an account, you just gave them an email and password that they could use to hack your other accounts. Bad!|
|Selecting Register for an account and log in.|The same question prompt will repeat.|
|Selecting Use a linked account (Google, Facebook etc.) to log in.|Brigette: DON'T DO THAT! Logging in with a linked account can give malicious actors permissions to your account that can lead to you losing your account.|
|(same as above)|The same question prompt will repeat.|
|Selecting Look for signs of your friend getting hacked, and don't click on that website any further.|[NPC] Brigette: That's right! You can try to reach out to your friend through other methods of contact to verify if it's really them. Even so, you should try and verify that the link is from a reputable website - google the domain name to make sure you're landing at the right website. [NPC] Brigette: Congratulations! You aced the test! [NPC] Brigette: Fill in the following information so that we can issue you a membership card.|
|(same as above)|Brigette will then open a menu, giving you a choice between filling out your name and personal details and refusing the card.|
|Signing the card|[NPC] Brigette: HEY! You should not be revealing personal or sensitive information! [NPC] Brigette: It is a bad idea to share your personal details to others online. NEW OBJECTIVE Talk to the Security Clerk|
|Refusing the card|[NPC] Brigette: Didn't fall for that one eh? Good job! NEW OBJECTIVE Talk to the Security Clerk|
|Interacting after leaving in the middle of the orientation|[NPC] Brigette: Great job with the briefing, now let's finish your orientation. [NPC] Brigette: Come this way. [NPC] Brigette: Here, take a seat.|
|Interacting after finishing the quiz, before claiming the [[Safety Badge]]|[NPC] Brigette: Great job with the quiz! Go speak with the Security Clerk for your security badge!|
|Interacting after finishing the quiz, before talking to [[Elizabeth]]|[NPC] Brigette: Not now, recruit — get yourself properly checked in first.|
|Interacting after finishing the quiz, after talking to [[Elizabeth]]|[NPC] Brigette: Stay safe, recruit!|
## Trivia
- If the player does not follow Brigette to a chair in the Briefing Room, they will be teleported straight onto the chair once she arrives there, unless they began dialogue with another [[NPC]].
  - Once on the chair, the player cannot move, dismount nor teleport out of it until the conversation is completed. They can, however, disconnect or warp into another island, in which case the conversation will be reset and started again once they return.

# Bruto
Aliases: Banker Bruto
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Bruto]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows players to access the [[Bank]].
Location: [[Dragontail Bank]]
Coordinates: -612.5 100 -786.5
Bruto is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Dragontail Bank]]. They manage the [[Bank|Banking System]].
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Bruto: Deposit your Coins before the Flaming Spiders get to you!|
## Banking System
Right-clicking Bruto will open the [[Bank]] interface. The player will be presented with several options, consisting of the ability to deposit and withdraw [[Coins]], viewing previous [[Bank|Banking]] activity, and upgrading the [[Bank]] account.
[[Coins]] stored in the Bank are not lost when the player [[Death|dies]].
### Bank Upgrades
Players are able to upgrade their bank account with [[Coins]] and [[Enchanted Gold Block|Enchanted Gold Blocks]] after meeting the respective requirement in the [[Gold Ingot|Gold]] Collection. Upgrading the [[Bank]] account increases the maximum amount of [[Coins]] able to be stored in the bank, and increases the maximum interest able to be gained. Upgrading Bank accounts also rewards [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
At least 10,000,000 [[Coins]] must be stored in the bank and the purse combined before the upgrade screen is unlocked.

Bruuh is an [[NPC]] in [[Dragontail]] that has a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] in his house. To claim the [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]], the player must pay Bruuh 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Bruuh: Hold it right there! [NPC] Bruuh: I have a Fairy Soul inside my property. If you want it you will have to pay! [NPC] Bruuh: You can get access to my house for 1,000,000 coins! [NPC] Bruuh: Do you want access? Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|Selecting [YES]|[NPC] Bruuh: Congratulations! You can now come and go as you please!|
|Selecting [NO]|[NPC] Bruuh: In that case you better get off my property before I call the guards!|
|Selecting [YES]; Without 1,000,000 [[Coins]]|[NPC] Bruuh: Are you trying to scam me, bruh? Get off my property!|
|Claiming Fairy Soul ([[Fairy Souls]]) Without Paying|[NPC] Bruuh: Nice try, bruh! Pay the toll to get the soul!|
|After Paying 1,000,000 [[Coins]]|[NPC] Bruuh: Once I found this Fairy Soul I decided to build my house around it.|
|After Paying 1,000,000 [[Coins]]|[NPC] Bruuh: I actually use the Fairy Soul as a lamp. They kinda glow in the dark a bit.|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 1/4)|[NPC] Bruuh: Are you a Wizard? [NPC] Bruuh: Well you don't look like one, and you don't smell like one either. [NPC] Bruuh: In that case, I have nothing against you. I wish you a sulphuric day sir.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 2/4)|[NPC] Bruuh: Hello stranger. Are you looking to join our faction? [NPC] Bruuh: What a fantastic choice! [NPC] Bruuh: We have a range of motives far deeper and more interesting than those terrible fire mages. [NPC] Bruuh: Joining our faction is quite hard but I'm sure an incredible individual like you will manage!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 3/4)|[NPC] Bruuh: Sul-phur [NPC] Bruuh: Sulphur? [NPC] Bruuh: Me Sulphur.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 4/4)|[NPC] Bruuh: Are you a Hero? [NPC] Bruuh: Did you come to free us from the everlasting threat of the wizards? [NPC] Bruuh: The mighty Volcano is protecting us, but for how long?|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Bruuh: You seem suspicious.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Bruuh: Sorry, I can't talk to you right now.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Bruuh: Please stop bothering me.|
|[[The Garden]] First Offer|[NPC] Bruuh: I have a business deal to propose to you. I have something that you need, and you have something that I need. Let's make a deal, shall we?|
|[[The Garden]] Subsequent Offers|[NPC] Bruuh: Yo.|
|[[The Garden]] First Completion|[NPC] Bruuh: I knew you would come to terms. Pleasure doing business with you.|
|[[The Garden]] Subsequent Completions|[NPC] Bruuh: Thanks.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying Before First Completion|[NPC] Bruuh: Are you trying to scam me, bruh? Get out of here! Oh wait, this is your house...I'll get out of here!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying After First Completion|[NPC] Bruuh: Okay, bye.|

- Paying Bruuh the 1,000,000 [[Coins]] unlocks the "Scam?" [[Achievements|achievement]].
- Bruuh as a [[Garden Visitors|Garden Visitor]], is the only visitor to only appear 1 time, unless invited again using [[Bachelor's Rose]].

# Button
Categories: Non-player characters

Button is an [[NPC]] in the [[Wizardman Bureau]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
Button serves as a source of information for how to call [[Wizardman]] into the [[Wizardman Bureau]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Press|[NPC] Button: Ouch! [NPC] Button: That tickles more than you'd think! [NPC] Button: A little gentleness goes a long way, you know? [NPC] Button: There's a RING hologram right above. Try using that, would you? [NPC] Button: Sigh, the things I endure... Youth these days indeed! Select an option: [I'm sorry.]|
|Selecting [I'm sorry.]|[NPC] Button: All forgiven. Just, maybe click on RING next time?|
|Clicking the RING hologram above|[NPC] Button: Ah, seeking Wizardman? They're currently on a cosmic errand. [NPC] Button: If you say "Help Wizardman!" I am sure they will come save you.|
|Pressing again before calling for [[Wizardman]]|[NPC] Button: That's the spot! Just kidding, please click the RING.|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Button: Ahhh! That actually hurts, you know!|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Button: You're really committed to this, aren't you? I can't wait to see what you don't do next.|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Button: What joy! Another press. I was just hoping you'd ignore the RING again.|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Button: It's almost like the RING doesn't exist for you. How poetic.|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Button: Congratulations! You've mastered the art of ignoring obvious instructions.|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Button: No, please, press me again. It's not like I had any plans today besides being your personal toy.|
|Pressing after calling for [[Wizardman]]|[NPC] Button: Why do you continue to do this? Wizardman is right there!|
|Pressing after calling for [[Wizardman]]|[NPC] Button: Okay, so you really do just want to torture me.|
|Pressing after calling for [[Wizardman]]|[NPC] Button: I guess it was pure cosmic randomness that you figured out how to ring Wizardman.|
## Trivia
- Another block-based [[NPC]] exists within the context of [[Wizardman]], that being [[Plate]].
- The RING hologram disappears after [[Wizardman]] appears.

# Calvin
Aliases: Returning Player Buffs, Returning Player Rewards
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Aura's Mayorship
[Calvin]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Grants buffs to returning players.
Location: [[Private Island]]
Calvin is an [[NPC]] that can appear on the [[Private Island]]. They offer rewards for players who return after 90+ days being offline.
## Usage
Interacting with Calvin allows the player to claim rewards and buffs. All buffs last for 7 days.
The following rewards and buffs are granted:
- 500,000–2,500,000 [[Coins]]
- +5–25 [[Wisdom|Global Wisdom]] (excluding XP from minions)
- +5–25% [[Slayer|Slayer XP]]
- +5–25% [[Dungeoneering|Dungeon XP]]
- +5–25% [[Classes|Dungeon Class XP]]
The buffs scale with offline time:
|Reward/Buff|Amount of days offline 90-120|Amount of days offline 120-150|Amount of days offline 150-180|Amount of days offline 180-210|Amount of days offline 210-240|Amount of days offline 240-270|Amount of days offline 270-300|Amount of days offline 300-330|Amount of days offline >330|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|

# Candidate Barry
Aliases: Barry (Old), Barry the Wizard, Mayor Barry
Categories: Non-player characters, Historical content, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
[Barry]
Also known as: Mayor Barry
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
Candidate Barry was significantly altered or removed after an update. This article is retained for historical purposes.
Barry the Wizard was a mayoral candidate. Their perks focused around [[Enchanting]] and [[Alchemy]].
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Barry may run for election with up to 3 possible perks.
  [[Candidate Barry]] Barry the Wizard
1. Arcane Catalyst: Spells deal 15% increased damage.
2. Astral Negotiator: Enchanting and anvils costs -15% less experience.
3. Magic XP Boost: Gain +15 [[Alchemy Wisdom]] and [[Enchanting Wisdom]]
Actual perks hidden until Barry is elected
1. Arcane Catalyst: Spells deal 15% increased damage. Secret Bonus: All abilities cost 15% less Mana
2. Astral Negotiator: Enchanting and anvils costs -15% less experience. Secret Bonus: Gain +1 [[Magic Find]] for every year that Barry has not been elected (max 20)
3. Magic XP Boost: Gain +15 [[Alchemy Wisdom]] and [[Enchanting Wisdom]]. Secret Bonus: Gain +2 Clicks in Superpairs Experiments
The perk "Astral Negotiator" mentioned "Enchanting" in general. It did not affect the [[Experimentation Table]].
## Dialogue
- The color of the NPC's name changes depending on which color they are in the current [[Mayor Election]]. Yellow has been used as a default color.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Barry: My wisdom knows no bounds, only I can bring balance. [NPC] Barry: Voting for me, you will. [NPC] Barry: My magic will grant you tremendous power.|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Barry: The other candidates are empty minded, can't you see I'm the only option?|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Barry: Powerful wizard, I am.|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Barry: I come from a faraway world, where everything is magic!|
## Gallery
- Barry without perks on the Alpha Network during Year 226
- Barry was not a candidate in the Alpha Network Year 225 election, but was still mayor
## Trivia
- His lines "Vote for me, you will" and "Powerful wizard, I am." are likely a reference to the way Yoda talks in the Star Wars franchise.
- He had been elected on the Alpha Server more than once before his first time in office on the Main Server.
- He helped to design the [[Starlight Armor]] and [[Starlight Wand]], as seen in [[Rosetta|Rosetta's UI]].
- On the 101st Mayor Election (for Mayor of Year 189):
  - Barry was elected for the first time.
    - Of all regular candidates, Barry was the last one to have been elected for the first time.
  - Some popular streamers and YouTubers decided to convince people to vote for Barry. Combined with other mayors having unfavorable perks during that election, Barry's voting rate was pushed higher.
  - Barry originally only had one perk in this election, but due to the attention given by the community, 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Skyerzz responded by changing Barry's perks to all three. This is the first time mayor changed perks during an election.
  - When elected, he revealed three Secret Bonuses on top of the three perks.
- In all elections which Barry won, the [[Wizard]]'s dialogue changed.
- He has retired from running for elections, instead he is mayor at [[Barry HQ]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
- Although Barry was no longer mayor on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Alpha Network]] as well, he appeared as a mayor in the year 226.
  - When he appeared as a mayor however, it displayed him as having no perks.
  - Also, during the election he was not actually a candidate.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 8, 2020|0.9|Added [[Barry]].|
|Feb 8, 2022|Secret Bonuses were added to Barry's perks.|
|Aug 13, 2022|Barry is no longer a mayor, and instead is in the [[Wizard Tower]].|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Although Barry is no longer mayor, Barry's Magic XP Boost perk changed from Gain 15% more [[Enchanting]] and [[Alchemy]] XP to Gain +15 [[Alchemy Wisdom]] and [[Enchanting Wisdom]]. This was revealed during [[Candidate Jerry/Perkpocalypse/Year 233]].|
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Replaced Candidate Barry with [[Barry]], who now lives in the [[Rift Dimension]].|
Linked from: [[Candidate Jerry]], [[Candidate Jerry/Perkpocalypse/Year 233]], [[Century Cakes]], [[Changelog/2020/September 8]], [[Magic Find]], [[Mayor Election]], [[Mayor Election/Events]], [[Wizard]]

# Candidate Jerry
Aliases: Jerry Mayor, Mayor Jerry
Categories: Non-player characters, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
See [[Candidate Jerry/Perkpocalypse]] for detailed Perkpocalypse time slots.
[Jerry]
Also known as: Mayor Jerry
Type: Mayor ([[Mayor Election]])
[Locations]
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]).
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6.5 79 19.5
Description: Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor.
Location: [[Election Room]]
Coordinates: -3.5 49 34.5
Jerry is a special Mayoral Candidate that, while elected, is located in the [[Community Center]], which can be found in front of the [[Hub Island|Hub]] spawn. Jerry is one of three special candidates that appear rarely.
## Mayoral Perks
Candidate Jerry is a special candidate, and will run for election according to the [[Mayor Election|Special Candidates Election Cycle]].
  [[Candidate Jerry]] Jerry
1. [[Candidate Jerry/Perkpocalypse]]: Activates all perks of another mayor every 18 SkyBlock days (6 hours).
2. Statspocalypse: Increases all [[Stats|Combat]] and [[Stats|Miscellaneous]] stats by 10%.
3. Jerrypocalypse: Reveal hidden Jerries from logging, farming, mining and killing mobs.
### Perkpocalypse
The Perkpocalypse ability will give a set of perks from another mayor, and it rotates every 6 Earth hours (18 SkyBlock days). Selected regular mayors are included in the Perkpocalypse rotation, ordered randomly each time Jerry is elected. See [[Candidate Jerry/Perkpocalypse]] for detailed Perkpocalypse time slots for each one of Jerry's mayor term.
Mayors that are included in the Perkpocalypse rotation are:
- [[Aatrox]]
- [[Cole]]
- [[Diana]]
- [[Finnegan]] (excluding [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]])
- [[Marina]]
- [[Paul]]
### Statspocalypse
This perk provides a 10% buff to the [[Stats|Combat]] and [[Stats|Miscellaneous]] stats (Additive (Additive buffs are added together with each other, and then the sum is multiplied with the flat amount.)).
### Jerrypocalypse
- Mining, farming crops, felling trees and killing mobs has a small chance to spawn a Green, Blue, Purple or Golden Jerry. When killing a [[Hidden Jerry]], it drops its respective [[Jerry Box]]. After spawning a Jerry, there is a 6 minute cooldown before another Jerry can spawn.
- Unlike most other similar events, the Jerrypocalypse will also trigger on players' [[Private Island|Private Islands]] from relevant activities.
- Your profile must have at least 4 hours of playtime to participate in Jerrypocalypse.
Items from Jerry Boxes are:
- [[Green Jerry Talisman]]
- [[Jerry Stone]]
- [[Runes|Jerry Rune]]
- [[Jerry Candy]]
- [[Jerry 3D Glasses]]
- [[Jerry-chine Gun]]
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None; [[Election Room]]|[NPC] Jerry: Jerry. [NPC] Jerry: Jerry? [NPC] Jerry: Jerry!|
## Trivia
- [[Diaz]] and [[Foxy]] are the only regular mayor candidates who are not in Jerry's Perkpocalypse mayor rotation.
- You cannot obtain the [[Ancestral Spade]] and [[Griffin Pet]] when [[Candidate Jerry]] Candidate Jerry has [[Diana]]'s perks.
- In SkyBlock Year 161, when Jerry was mayor and had the Perkpocalypse mayor rotation of [[Cole]], some lobbies in the [[Dwarven Mines]] still had the [[Mining Fiesta]] perks active even though it ended.
- In SkyBlock Year 233 and 257, although [[Candidate Barry|Barry]] is no longer a mayor candidate, when Jerry was mayor, [[Candidate Barry]] was still in [[Candidate Jerry/Perkpocalypse|the Perkpocalypse Mayor Rotation]].
  - Hidden perks were not shown for on the Perkpocalypse mayor rotation of [[Barry]]. His hidden perks are believed to be not active.

# Captain Baha
Aliases: Baha, Steadfast
Categories: Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Fishing, Quest giver
[Captain Baha]
Type: [[NPC]]

[Locations]

Captain Baha is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Fisherman's Hut]] who allows players to access the [[Ship]]. He can also be found on the Ship in the [[Backwater Bayou]] and [[Lotus Atoll]].
## Purpose
Captain Baha allows the player to access the [[Ship]], after reaching [[Fishing|Fishing V (5)]] and bringing him the [[Rusty Ship Engine]]. The ship features the [[Ship|Ship Navigator]], which can be used to visit various [[Fishing]] islands.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|If you haven't spoken to [[Fisherwoman Enid]]|[NPC] Captain Baha: Before you can sail, you must prove you know how to fish. [NPC] Captain Baha: Go find Fisherwoman Enid, learn how to fish, and then we'll talk!|
|Before Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]])|[NPC] Captain Baha: These waters are rough, matey! [NPC] Captain Baha: You'll need at least Fishing Skill V before setting sail!|
|Trying to open the Ship Navigator before reaching Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]])|[NPC] Captain Baha: Ahoy, sailor! You must be Fishing Skill V to navigate the seas!|
|Trying to open the Ship Navigator before talking to [[Fisherman Gerald]]|[NPC] Captain Baha: This Ship is missing an engine! Fisherman Gerald might have a clue on where to find it.|
|After Fishing V (5) ([[Fishing]])|[NPC] Captain Baha: I was about to set sail, but this Ship is missing its engine! [NPC] Captain Baha: Maybe Fisherman Gerald knows where it is?|
|After bringing [[Rusty Ship Engine]]|[NPC] Captain Baha: Ahoy, is that the Rusty Ship Engine? Select an option: [Yes!]|
|After selecting [Yes!]|[NPC] Captain Baha: Excellent! I'm the Captain of this Ship, which means I oversee everything from repairs to navigation. [NPC] Captain Baha: You can access the Ship Upgrades menu by talking to me. [NPC] Captain Baha: Apply that Rusty Ship Engine in the Engine slot by clicking the engine in your inventory!|
|Trying to open the Ship Navigator before applying the [[Rusty Ship Engine]]|[NPC] Captain Baha: Apply the Rusty Ship Engine in the Ship Upgrades menu, and then we can set sail!|
|After applying the [[Rusty Ship Engine]]|[NPC] Captain Baha: Amazing! That'll do just the trick! [NPC] Captain Baha: It's a pretty weak engine, but it will be just powerful enough to reach the Backwater Bayou. [NPC] Captain Baha: Now, before we set off, you should make sure you've bought some starter equipment from the Fishing Merchant in the Fishing Outpost. [NPC] Captain Baha: And when you're ready, use the Ship Navigator to travel to the Bayou!|
|After arriving at the [[Backwater Bayou]]|[NPC] Captain Baha: Land ho! We have arrived at the Backwater Bayou! [NPC] Captain Baha: You go on and explore! Come back to the Ship and set sail when you're done!|
|First interaction after reaching Fishing X (10) ([[Fishing]])|[NPC] Captain Baha: If you want to conquer the seven seas and be the first sailor to reach World's End, we're going to need a bigger crew! [NPC] Captain Baha: I've given it some thought, and I think we should recruit some more sailors to help us out. [NPC] Captain Baha: You can find the Ship Crew menu by talking to me. [NPC] Captain Baha: Use it to find NPCs who might want to join us on our adventure! [NPC] Captain Baha: If you find someone that interests you, persuade them to join the crew!|
|After arriving at the [[Lotus Atoll]]|[NPC] Captain Baha: Land ho! We have landed onto the Lotus Atoll! [NPC] Captain Baha: You go on and explore! Come back to the Ship and set sail when you're done!|
## Trivia
- Captain Baha's name could be inspired by Captain Ahab from the novel Moby-Dick.

# Carnival Cowboy

Carnival Cowboy is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Carnival]] during the [[Carnival]] event. They let the player compete in the [[Carnival|Zombie Shootout]] minigame.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Howdy there, partner! [NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Interested in playin' Zombie Shootout, are ya? [NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Ye'll get more score based on what armor the Zombie. [NPC] Carnival Cowboy: There'll also be sum Redstone Lamps aroun' the arena that can be shot fer points when they light up! [NPC] Carnival Cowboy: That all make sense to ya? Select an option: 🡺 [Sure does, partner! I'd like to give it a try.] 🡺 [I think I'll save this one for later.]|
|Subsequent Interactions|[NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Howdy there, partner! [NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Wouldja like to play Zombie Shootout? Select an option: 🡺 [Sure thing, partner!] 🡺 [Could ya tell me the rules again?] 🡺 [I'd like to do somthin' else fer now.]|
|Selecting randomized Green Dialogue Option; (e.g. [Sure thing, partner!]); with 5 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|[NPC] Carnival Cowboy: I won't keep ya, then. [NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Good luck, pal!|
|Selecting randomized Green Dialogue Option; (e.g. [Sure thing, partner!]); without 5 Carnival Tickets ([[Carnival Ticket]])|[NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Sorry pal, but ya need 5x Carnival Tickets to play! [NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Guess we'll have to save it fer later.|

[NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Ye'll get more score based on what armor the Zombie's wearin'. [NPC] Carnival Cowboy: There'll also be sum Redstone Lamps aroun' the arena that can be shot fer points when they light up! [NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Are ya ready to play now? Select an option: 🡺 [More ready than a rooster at dawn!] 🡺 [Reckon I'll wrangle up a fresh thought instead.]|
|Selecting randomized Red Dialogue Option; (e.g. [I'd like to do somthin' else fer now.])|[NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Well, gosh darn it! [NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Stop wasting my time then, will ya?|
|Selecting randomized Green Dialogue Option; (e.g. [Sure thing, partner!]); after the [[Carnival]] has ended|[NPC] Carnival Cowboy: Aw shucks, the Carnival is about to close! Hope to see yer next time.|

# Carnival Fisherman
Categories: Non-player characters
[Carnival Fisherman]
Type: [[NPC]]
Description: Allows the player to play the Catch a Fish ([[Carnival]]) minigame in the [[Carnival]].
Location: [[Carnival]]
Coordinates: -81.5 72 29.5
Carnival Fisherman is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Carnival]] during the [[Carnival]] event. They let the player compete in the [[Carnival|Catch a Fish]] minigame.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction None|[NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Welcome to Catch a Fish, friend! [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Mayor Foxy graciously allowed me to use this pond for my attraction. [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Anyways, the rules of the game are as follows: [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: You will be given 100s to catch some fish with a fishing rod I will provide you. [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Each type of fish has a different color and point value. [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Try to catch as many as you can before the time runs out. [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: So, interested in playing?|
|First Interaction Selecting [I sure am!] but without 5 [[Carnival Ticket]]|[NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Sorry friend, but it looks like you don't have the 5x Carnival Tickets required to play... [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Maybe next time.|
|First Interaction Selecting [I sure am!] with 5 [[Carnival Ticket]]|[NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Well, here goes... [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Good luck, friend!|
|First Interaction Selecting [Maybe later.]|[NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Alas, not everyone can appreciate the art of fishing... [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: I'll be waiting here if you change your mind again.|
|Subsequent Interactions None|[NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Welcome to Catch a Fish, friend! [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Are you here to play? Select an option: 🡺 [You guessed it!] 🡺 [Could you tell me the rules again?] 🡺 [Nope.]|
|Subsequent Interactions Selecting; [You guessed it!]; or [Let's do it!]; but without; 5 [[Carnival Ticket]]|[NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Sorry friend, but it looks like you don't have the 5x Carnival Tickets required to play... [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Maybe next time.|
|Subsequent Interactions Selecting; [You guessed it!]; or [Let's do it!]; with 5 [[Carnival Ticket]]|[NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Well, here goes... [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Good luck, friend!|
|Subsequent Interactions Selecting [Could you tell me the rules again?]|[NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Of course! [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: You will be given 100s to catch some fish with a fishing rod I will provide you. [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Each type of fish has a different color and point value. [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Try to catch as many as you can before the time runs out. [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Would you like to play? Select an option: 🡺 [Let's do it!] 🡺 [I think I'll reconsider.]|
|Subsequent Interactions Selecting [Nope.]; or [I think I'll reconsider.]|[NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Alas, not everyone can appreciate the art of fishing... [NPC] Carnival Fisherman: I'll be waiting here if you change your mind.|
|Subsequent Interactions Selecting [You guessed it!] after the [[Carnival]] has ended|[NPC] Carnival Fisherman: Aww, the Carnival is closing for the year now. There's always a next time!|
|Subsequent Interactions Selecting [You guessed it!] if the server is lagging|[NPC] Carnival Fisherman: This server's as slow as reeling in a heavy catch, can't play the game like this. Sorry, friend!|
